## Notes

- `pipecat`: packages/core/src/index.ts: version() plus the import-time version banner, which is skipped for the pipecat/pc CLI (checks process.argv[1]). The version is hardcoded to the core package version (0.0.0); upstream reads the pipecat-ai distribution metadata. Not ported, because they are Python packaging specific: the check for a separately installed pipecat-ai-flows, and the wait_for2 patch for Python < 3.12. The banner is an import side effect, while package.json says sideEffects:false. | UNRESOLVED: audio/krisp_instance.ts: the krisp_audio SDK is a stub until setKrispAudioSdk() is called. LocalSmartTurnAnalyzerV3 needs an injected createSession (an ONNX runtime), and the PIPECAT_SMART_TURN_LOG_DATA WAV dump is a stub that only logs a warning because core has no filesystem. | UNRESOLVED: adapters/schemas/direct_function.ts: upstream sets each parameter's 'description' from the docstring. The TS metadata (DirectFunctionMetadata) has no per-parameter description field, so descriptions only appear if they are put inside the JSON-schema hint. Also, DirectFunctionWrapper.invoke silently drops extra argument names, where Python's function(params=..., **args) raises TypeError. Fixing either would mean inventing API, so I left both. | UNRESOLVED: audio/turn/smart_turn/base_smart_turn.ts: an edge case remains. If sampleRate is 0, maxSamples is 0; Python's segment_audio[-0:] keeps the whole array, while the TS slice gives an empty one. It only happens if setSampleRate was never called, so I left it. | UNRESOLVED: audio/utils.ts: the reimplemented ITU-R BS.1770 integratedLoudness (replacing the Python 'loudness' package) and the float32 accumulation in the whisper-feature normalization could not be checked numerically against upstream here. No tests exist for these modules.
- `pipecat.adapters`: Upstream __init__.py is empty, so index.ts only has the header and `export {}`. | UNRESOLVED: Minor, left as is: Python's json.dumps escapes non-ASCII and JSON.stringify does not, so the wire bytes differ but parse to the same values. JSONMessageSerializer serializes a dataclass from Object.entries, not dataclasses.fields, and passes every decoded field to the constructor instead of splitting init and non-init fields. str()/repr() of None, floats and bools in classifier error messages and in OpenAI Decisions probability keys does not match Python exactly (for example 'None' vs '', 1.0 vs 1).
- `pipecat.adapters.base_llm_adapter`: Dropped as deprecated: the __init_subclass__ warning for a sync get_llm_invocation_params. - `pipecat.adapters.schemas.direct_function`: DIVERGENCE: JS has no runtime type hints or docstrings. Parameter names and required-ness (no default value) are parsed from the function source. The description and per-parameter types come from optional metadata on the function (DirectFunctionMetadata: description, and parameters as JSON schemas, JS constructors, null, or unions given as arrays). Parameters with no metadata get {}. Minified functions lose their parameter names. invoke(args, params) passes args by matching names to the parsed parameters. The async check uses constructor.name === 'AsyncFunction'. tool_options became toolOptions(fn, opts?) or toolOptions(opts)(fn), which sets _pipecatCancelOnInterruption, _pipecatTimeoutSecs and _pipecatCancellableByLlm. llm_service.ts reads these same names. | UNRESOLVED: audio/krisp_instance.ts: the krisp_audio SDK is a stub until setKrispAudioSdk() is called. - `pipecat.adapters.services`: Empty upstream __init__.py; index.ts is `export {}`. | UNRESOLVED: Minor, left as is: Python's json.dumps escapes non-ASCII and JSON.stringify does not, so the wire bytes differ but parse to the same values. - `pipecat.adapters.services.anthropic_adapter`: Port is at packages/anthropic/src/anthropic_adapter.ts. Nothing upstream is deprecated, so nothing was dropped. tsc shows no errors in this file and biome is clean. No @anthropic-ai/sdk types are imported: MessageParam, TextBlockParam, ToolUnionParam and CacheControlEphemeralParam are declared structurally, as open_ai_adapter.ts does. ANTHROPIC_NOT_GIVEN is undefined and anthropicIsGiven is a type guard. getLlmInvocationParams(context, options) takes enablePromptCaching (required), systemInstruction, ensureLastMessageIsUser and systemPromptCacheTtl in one options object. The nested dataclass ConvertedMessages is exported at top level and also exposed as the static AnthropicLLMAdapter.ConvertedMessages. Python copy.deepcopy becomes structuredClone and json.loads becomes pyJsonLoads. Python's `tools or []` is handled with an isGiven-aware helper, because the TS NOT_GIVEN is a truthy object. Conversion errors are wrapped in LLMContextConversionError after rethrowIfCancelled. I created packages/anthropic (package.json, tsconfig.json). Another agent has since added the @anthropic-ai/sdk dependency to package.json, and I left it in place. pipecat.runtimes is [] until compat is run.
- `pipecat.adapters.services.aws_nova_sonic_adapter`: Ported 1:1; tool schemas serialized with pyJsonDumps.
- `pipecat.adapters.services.azure_voice_live_adapter`: packages/azure/src/azure_voice_live_adapter.ts. Packed-history text uses JSON.stringify(..., null, 2), as the OpenAI realtime adapter port (pyJsonDumps has no indent).
- `pipecat.adapters.services.bedrock_adapter`: Inline bytes kept as Uint8Array; the service base64-encodes them on the wire as boto3 does.
- `pipecat.adapters.services.deepseek_adapter`: packages/deepseek/src/deepseek_adapter.ts. Tested by packages/deepseek/test/test_deepseek_adapter.test.ts (TestDeepSeekGetLLMInvocationParams of tests/test_get_llm_invocation_params.py).
- `pipecat.adapters.services.gemini_adapter`: Ported in full to /Users/pol/vscode/pipedog/packages/google/src/gemini_adapter.ts. Nothing upstream is deprecated, so nothing was dropped. Differences from upstream: (1) Messages are @google/genai Content/Part plain objects, so part fields use camelCase (inlineData, functionCall, thoughtSignature). The getMessagesForLogging copy has camelCase keys too, not the Python SDK's snake_case to_json_dict(). (2) The JS SDK carries bytes as base64 strings, so inline data and thought signatures are base64-encoded. prefersRawFileBytes stays true, so the resolver still caches raw bytes. The placeholder signature is the base64 of 'skip_thought_signature_validator'. A bookmark signature given as a Uint8Array is base64-encoded before it is applied. (3) Inline-data bookmark matching compares decoded byte lengths and accepts either base64 text or a Uint8Array. (4) Part.from_uri without a MIME type uses a local extension table in place of Python's mimetypes.guess_type, and throws 'Failed to determine mime type for file: ...' like upstream when the type is unknown. (5) The nested dataclasses (ConvertedMessages, MessageConversionResult, MessageConversionParams) are classes at module level and are also static members of GeminiLLMAdapter. (6) The (note, tool) warned-pairs set is keyed by a JSON string. tsc and biome pass for this file. | UNRESOLVED: gemini_adapter.ts _thoughtSignatureBookmarkMatchesPart logs the unknown bookmark with JSON.stringify where upstream uses a Python dict repr. Only the format of a warning log line differs, and core has no pyRepr helper, so I left it. | UNRESOLVED: gemini_adapter.ts _addPlaceholderThoughtSignatures and _applyThoughtSignaturesToMessages: upstream filters with isinstance(message, Content). The JS SDK's Content is a plain object with no class, so the port cannot tell a raw dict from Content and treats any object as Content. This cannot be fixed without a class to check against.
- `pipecat.adapters.services.gemini_live_adapter`: Ported in full to /Users/pol/vscode/pipedog/packages/google/src/gemini_live_adapter.ts. getLlmInvocationParams takes { systemInstruction, serviceTools } and uses _realtimeSessionTools(...) ?? NOT_GIVEN. _convertToolCallsToText is a protected static. One small difference: in the '[Function X returned ...]' text, non-string tool content is stringified by JS template rules, not Python's repr. tsc and biome pass for this file. | UNRESOLVED: gemini_adapter.ts _thoughtSignatureBookmarkMatchesPart logs the unknown bookmark with JSON.stringify where upstream uses a Python dict repr. Only the format of a warning log line differs, and core has no pyRepr helper, so I left it. | UNRESOLVED: gemini_adapter.ts _addPlaceholderThoughtSignatures and _applyThoughtSignaturesToMessages: upstream filters with isinstance(message, Content). The JS SDK's Content is a plain object with no class, so the port cannot tell a raw dict from Content and treats any object as Content. This cannot be fixed without a class to check against.
- `pipecat.adapters.services.grok_realtime_adapter`: DIVERGENCE: implemented in packages/xai/src/grok_realtime_adapter.ts (next to the service that uses it; @pipedog/grok depends on @pipedog/xai, not the reverse); packages/grok/src/grok_realtime_adapter.ts re-exports it.
- `pipecat.adapters.services.inworld_realtime_adapter`: packages/inworld/src/inworld_realtime_adapter.ts. Packed history uses JSON.stringify(indent 2), like the OpenAI Realtime adapter port (integral floats print as ints).
- `pipecat.adapters.services.mistral_adapter`: packages/mistral/src/mistral_adapter.ts (copy.deepcopy -> structuredClone). No upstream test; covered through test/mistral_llm_client.test.ts.
- `pipecat.adapters.services.open_ai_adapter`: Core does not depend on the openai SDK, so the ChatCompletion* types are declared structurally. OPENAI_NOT_GIVEN is undefined, which is how the JS SDK omits a field, and openaiIsGiven checks for !== undefined. getLlmInvocationParams(context, {systemInstruction?, convertDeveloperToUser}). | UNRESOLVED: audio/krisp_instance.ts: the krisp_audio SDK is a stub until setKrispAudioSdk() is called. The TS metadata (DirectFunctionMetadata) has no per-parameter description field, so descriptions only appear if they are put inside the JSON-schema hint. Also, DirectFunctionWrapper.invoke silently drops extra argument names, where Python's function(params=..., **args) raises TypeError. Fixing either would mean inventing API, so I left both. | UNRESOLVED: audio/turn/smart_turn/base_smart_turn.ts: an edge case remains. If sampleRate is 0, maxSamples is 0; Python's segment_audio[-0:] keeps the whole array, while the TS slice gives an empty one. - `pipecat.audio`: Empty upstream __init__.py; index.ts is `export {}`. | UNRESOLVED: Minor, left as is: Python's json.dumps escapes non-ASCII and JSON.stringify does not, so the wire bytes differ but parse to the same values. JSONMessageSerializer serializes a dataclass from Object.entries, not dataclasses.fields, and passes every decoded field to the constructor instead of splitting init and non-init fields. str()/repr() of None, floats and bools in classifier error messages and in OpenAI Decisions probability keys does not match Python exactly (for example 'None' vs '', 1.0 vs 1).

- `pipecat.bus.serializers.json`: The wire format is upstream's. DIVERGENCES: (1) Python resolves the qualified class names in __type__ with importlib. TS uses a module-level registry instead, with two exported functions I added: registerSerializableType(qualifiedName, type) and registerEnumField(cls, field, enumObj). Registered by default: every class in frames/frames, bus/messages, bus/ui/messages, metrics/metrics and registry/types, plus LLMContext, LLMSpecificMessage, ToolsSchema, FunctionSchema, FrameDirection and ErrorCategory. An instance of an unregistered class is skipped with upstream's 'unserializable type' warning. (2) Dataclass field names are converted camelCase -> snake_case on the wire and back; plain dict keys are not changed. (3) An `as const` enum value cannot say which enum it belongs to, so plain-Enum fields have to be declared. BusFrameMessage.direction is declared by default. Class enums such as ErrorCategory are detected automatically. (4) Pydantic models and dataclasses are serialized the same way. (5) Deserialization passes all decoded fields to the options constructor, then assigns them to the instance (this covers init=False fields). (6) JSON.stringify does not escape non-ASCII the way Python's ensure_ascii does; the payloads parse to the same data. The type adapters come from core's own bus/adapters (../adapters/index.ts). | UNRESOLVED: Minor, left as is: Python's json.dumps escapes non-ASCII and JSON.stringify does not, so the wire bytes differ but parse to the same values. JSONMessageSerializer serializes a dataclass from Object.entries, not dataclasses.fields, and passes every decoded field to the constructor instead of splitting init and non-init fields. str()/repr() of None, floats and bools in classifier error messages and in OpenAI Decisions probability keys does not match Python exactly (for example 'None' vs '', 1.0 vs 1).
- `pipecat.bus.subscriber`: Split into an interface BusSubscriber (name plus methods), an interface BusSubscriberMethods, and a defineMixin factory BusSubscriber(Base) with the default acceptsBusMessage (returns true) and a no-op onBusMessage. DIVERGENCE: rich's Live dashboard is a plain-ANSI redraw-in-place table on a TTY and streamed lines otherwise; python-dotenv is a small .env loader (search upward, never overrides). Deprecated --stop-bot and --trigger-disconnect not ported.
- `pipecat.clocks`: Empty upstream __init__.py; index.ts is `export {}`. | UNRESOLVED: Minor, left as is: Python's json.dumps escapes non-ASCII and JSON.stringify does not, so the wire bytes differ but parse to the same values. JSONMessageSerializer serializes a dataclass from Object.entries, not dataclasses.fields, and passes every decoded field to the constructor instead of splitting init and non-init fields. str()/repr() of None, floats and bools in classifier error messages and in OpenAI Decisions probability keys does not match Python exactly (for example 'None' vs '', 1.0 vs 1).
- `pipecat.clocks.base_clock`: hand-ported reference
- `pipecat.clocks.system_clock`: hand-ported reference
- `pipecat.evals`: index.ts re-exports the main API (pipedog addition; upstream __init__ is empty). The suite is left out (Node-only).
- `pipecat.evals.__main__`: The pipedog-eval bin (package.json bin), guarded so importing it has no side effects.
- `pipecat.evals._session_subprocess`: Runs one scenario in its own process: spawned with process.execPath on this module (.ts in the repo, .js when published); main() runs only when it is the entry point. Result written with pyJsonDumps(result.toDict()).
- `pipecat.evals.audio`: loadUserAudio(path, {decoder}) -> [pcm, sampleRate]. DIVERGENCE: soundfile is replaced by a pure-TS WAV reader (PCM 8/16/24/32, float 32/64, extensible); other formats need a decoder (setUserAudioDecoder or the decoder option).
- `pipecat.evals.base_driver`: Abstract BaseEvalDriver<R>({client, stream, judge, trace, progress}); result() takes one options object.
- `pipecat.evals.client`: EvalClient(botUrl, {stream, trace, params, sessionParams, userTts, botStt, persona}) on core Pipeline/PipelineWorker/WorkerRunner and EvalClientTransport; Silero loaded lazily from @pipedog/audio-silero. Dropped deprecated BOT_READY_TIMEOUT_S alias and the trigger_disconnect session check. DIVERGENCE: waitForBot probes TCP with lazy node:net (not on Workers); a refused connection is a ConnectionClosed or an Error with a code (no InvalidHandshake/OSError); sendImage uses a small extension-to-MIME table.
- `pipecat.evals.client_transport`: EvalClientTransport(uri, params, {flags, recorder}). The recorder writes WAV with core pcmToWav via lazy node:fs/promises; _RecorderTrack takes an injectable clock.
- `pipecat.evals.conversation`: Full port. Messages are plain objects with unchanged keys; strip() uses Python's isspace set.
- `pipecat.evals.conversation_observer`: Full port; event "on_conversation_ended" unchanged.
- `pipecat.evals.events`: EvalEventStream({botAudio, trace}); event dicts keep snake_case keys. Upstream has no test for it.
- `pipecat.evals.fastapi_transport`: EvalFastAPIWebsocketTransport(websocket, params, flags, {inputName, outputName}) on pipedog's bring-your-own-socket FastAPIWebsocketTransport.
- `pipecat.evals.harness`: skipped: deprecated alias module (moved to session and script_session since 1.9.0).
- `pipecat.evals.judge`: EvalJudge(classifier, {explainer, explainBelow, allowContinue, maxTokens}); fromConfig() is async (DIVERGENCE). Dropped deprecated: LLM service as classifier, service= keyword, evaluate_run(transcript=).
- `pipecat.evals.matcher`: ExpectationMatcher({stream, judge, trace}); judge calls tagged with contextualize({eval_pipeline: "judge"}). Full port.
- `pipecat.evals.persona`: Full port. new EvalPersona(description, goal, llm) stays positional (required args).
- `pipecat.evals.results`: Dropped deprecated EvalTurnResult, EvalResult, EvalTurnProgress. Dataclasses are classes with options constructors; toDict() writes dataclasses.asdict's snake_case keys, static fromDict() mirrors suite.py's _result_from_dict. pyFormatFixed() reproduces Python's :.Nf (half-to-even).
- `pipecat.evals.scenario`: Dropped deprecated load_scenario_file, the EvalScenario/EvalTurn re-exports and the flat file shape (DIVERGENCE: a flat file fails with "'scenarios:' must be a non-empty list"). EvalScenarioFile.load, isScenarioFile and _loadMapping are async (lazy node:fs); file[name] -> file.get(name), len -> length. YAML through core utils/yaml SafeLoader with !include.
- `pipecat.evals.scenario_config`: Dropped deprecated trigger_disconnect key (ignored when present). describeConfig(scenario, {color}). _DEFAULT_LLM_MODELS has no openai entry (service: openai is deprecated).
- `pipecat.evals.script`: Dropped deprecated EvalTurn, EvalScenario, EvalScriptScenario.load, trigger_disconnect. Options constructors with camelCase fields. _parseScript is async (resolves audio:/image: paths like Path.resolve via lazy node:fs). DIVERGENCE: JS has no int/float distinction, so integral floats (pause_ms: 2000.0) are accepted.
- `pipecat.evals.script_driver`: EvalScriptDriver({scenario, defaultTimeoutMs, ...}). Full port.
- `pipecat.evals.script_session`: EvalScriptSession(scenario, botUrl, {params, judge, userTts, botStt}) and async fromScenario. Dropped deprecated on_progress callback, knob keywords, trigger_disconnect passthrough.
- `pipecat.evals.serializer`: Dropped deprecated RTVIEvalSerializer. EvalConnectionFlags has modelDump()/modelValidate() (pydantic lax bools); evalConnectMessage() writes compact JSON. EvalSerializer takes an imageDecoder option. DIVERGENCE: Pillow is replaced by a built-in PNG decoder (DecompressionStream); JPEG and other formats need an injected imageDecoder.
- `pipecat.evals.services`: Builders are async (DIVERGENCE: factory resolved with dynamic import(); "module.export" where module is a package, file: URL or path, or a function from code). Ollama imported lazily. DIVERGENCE: Kokoro, Moonshine and local Whisper are not ported; their builders throw naming the service (the default audio services therefore need a factory). Dropped deprecated service: cartesia / service: openai and DEFAULT_OPENAI_MODEL. Shared helpers in src/_pyutil.ts (sync SHA-256, Python repr/float formatting).
- `pipecat.evals.session`: EvalSessionParams (options constructor) and abstract EvalSession<R> extends BaseObject with "on_progress". fromScenario(scenario, botUrl, {params, personaLlm, judge, userTts, botStt}) is async (service builders are) with one union signature. Dropped deprecated: stop_bot/trigger_disconnect params, _params_with_deprecated_knobs and the knob keywords, the on_progress callback argument. DIVERGENCE: Node socket errors (Error with code) count as OSError, named after the Python class (ECONNREFUSED -> ConnectionRefusedError); the trace prints the JS stack after "Traceback (most recent call last):".
- `pipecat.evals.simulation`: Dropped deprecated EvalSimulationScenario.load and trigger_disconnect. describeSimulation(sim, {color}). DIVERGENCE: integral floats accepted where ints are required.
- `pipecat.evals.simulation_driver`: EvalSimulationDriver({simulation, persona, ...}). Dropped deprecated transcript().
- `pipecat.evals.simulation_judge`: EvalSimulationJudge(simulation, {judge, explainBelow, trace}); _buildJudge async (EvalJudge.fromConfig is).
- `pipecat.evals.simulation_session`: EvalSimulationSession(scenario, botUrl, {params, personaLlm, judge, userTts, botStt}) and async fromScenario. Dropped deprecated trigger_disconnect passthrough.
- `pipecat.evals.suite`: Dropped deprecated: bare-string runner_body, EvalSuite.run(on_update=, use_cache=, default_timeout_ms=) with _params_with_deprecated_knobs, stop_bot/trigger_disconnect. EvalRun/EvalManifest are classes with options constructors; EvalManifest.load, run.load and suite.run are async. Node/Bun only (node:child_process, node:fs). DIVERGENCE: capturePipelineLogs(logsDir, prefix, {name, enabled}, fn) takes a callback; loguru's contextualize/extra -> contextualize()/logContext() on AsyncLocalStorage (_log_context.ts) plus a logger sink; bots spawn with process.execPath ("{python}" -> bun/node) and @pipedog/runner flags; asyncio.Condition built from Futures; small shlex.split/str.format replacements.
- `pipecat.evals.transport`: Only EvalTransportParams. Dropped deprecated: the EvalTransport server and what only it used (query-param constants and helpers, server-based EvalInputTransport/EvalOutputTransport).
- `pipecat.evals.tts`: CachingTTSService(inner, {cacheKey, cacheDir, useCache}). Disk cache via lazy node:fs/promises and core pcmToWav/readWav. DIVERGENCE: DEFAULT_CACHE_DIR from $HOME/%USERPROFILE% via getEnv; StartFrame has no fields in pipedog, so the inner service gets the sample rate and metrics-off through its FrameProcessorSetup.
- `pipecat.flows`: Index re-exports (as the former pipecat-flows package did). DIVERGENCE: NodeConfig/ActionConfig keys are camelCase while FlowConfig (YAML/JSON) keeps snake_case; consolidated results are two-element arrays; direct functions take flowManager as first parameter with metadata for description/parameters; handlers are called as (args, flowManager); FlowConfig.fromFile and !include use an injected readFile; validation is hand-written and raises ValidationError; placeholders render with String()/JSON; no return-annotation warning. Deprecated APIs not ported, except RESET_WITH_SUMMARY which warns once.
- `pipecat.flows.actions`: DIVERGENCE: camelCase ActionConfig keys (appendTextToContext); handlers called as (action, flowManager).
- `pipecat.flows.adapters`: DIVERGENCE: the summary prompt interpolates JSON of the message list instead of the Python repr.
- `pipecat.flows.config`: DIVERGENCE: hand-written validation raising ValidationError (not Pydantic); camelCase fields with snake_case file format and modelDump(); injected readFile for fromFile and !include; shipped flow_config.schema.json is generated from modelJsonSchema().
- `pipecat.flows.exceptions`: No divergences.
- `pipecat.flows.flow`: DIVERGENCE: no return-annotation warning (no runtime annotations); handlers called as (args, flowManager) and return two-element arrays; handlers namespaces are objects or Maps.
- `pipecat.flows.manager`: DIVERGENCE: camelCase NodeConfig; placeholders render with String()/JSON, not str(); flowManager naming. RESET_WITH_SUMMARY kept, warns once.
- `pipecat.flows.types`: DIVERGENCE: camelCase NodeConfig/ActionConfig; two-element array results; flowManager first parameter with attached metadata. Deprecated FlowResult, flows_direct_function, legacy handler aliases and role_messages not ported.
- `pipecat.frames`: Empty upstream __init__.py; index.ts is `export {}`. | UNRESOLVED: Minor, left as is: Python's json.dumps escapes non-ASCII and JSON.stringify does not, so the wire bytes differ but parse to the same values. JSONMessageSerializer serializes a dataclass from Object.entries, not dataclasses.fields, and passes every decoded field to the constructor instead of splitting init and non-init fields. str()/repr() of None, floats and bools in classifier error messages and in OpenAI Decisions probability keys does not match Python exactly (for example 'None' vs '', 1.0 vs 1).
- `pipecat.frames.frames`: Continued below the PORTING marker in packages/core/src/frames/frames.ts, marker removed. Every non-deprecated frame and helper class is ported. Added mixins: FileRawFrame and a marker DTMFFrame. InputTextRawFrame extends SystemFrame, copies TextFrame's fields and uses addVirtualBase(TextFrame). Re-exports TextType because upstream rtvi/observer imports it through frames. Deprecated items dropped: UninterruptibleFrame; FatalErrorFrame; ErrorFrame.fatal (toString leaves out 'fatal:', and the RTVI port must send fatal=false); all StartFrame fields (audio_in/out_sample_rate, enable_metrics/tracing/usage_metrics, report_only_initial_ttfb, tracing_context), since processors read them from FrameProcessorSetup and StartFrame now has no fields; AggregatedTextFrame/AggregatedTextProgressFrame.aggregated_by; the TTSSpeakFrame(append_to_context=None) coercion; ServiceUpdateSettingsFrame.settings (the deprecated dict; only delta/service/reachInactiveServices kept); TaskFrame, TaskSystemFrame, EndTaskFrame, StopTaskFrame, CancelTaskFrame and InterruptionTaskFrame aliases; the AggregationType alias. Divergences: OutputDTMFFrame/OutputDTMFUrgentFrame.to_string() is renamed toDialString(), because toString() is __str__. fromString() rejects invalid characters with a plain Error (upstream raises ValueError from KeypadEntry). The LLMThoughtStartFrame and DTMF validations throw Error. toString prints JS null/true instead of None/True, and image sizes print as '(w, h)'. Still failing the typecheck: missing modules adapters/schemas/direct_function.ts and transcriptions/language.ts, which other agents have not written yet, plus TS6305 from the @pipedog/asyncio project reference (packages/asyncio/dist not built), which every importer of that package gets.
- `pipecat.frames.protobufs.frames_pb2`: DIVERGENCE: upstream is protoc-generated code that needs the protobuf runtime. This is a hand-written proto3 codec for the same frames.proto schema, with the same wire format. Messages: TextFrame, AudioRawFrame, TranscriptionFrame, MessageFrame, InterruptionFrame, Frame. Each has camelCase fields, serializeToString(), static fromString() and parseFromString(); Frame has whichOneof('frame'), and setting one oneof member clears the others. DESCRIPTOR.fieldsByName[...].messageType.fields lists each field's proto name and its TS property (attr). uint64 values are JS numbers, exact up to 2^53. An unset optional pts reads null. | UNRESOLVED: Minor, left as is: Python's json.dumps escapes non-ASCII and JSON.stringify does not, so the wire bytes differ but parse to the same values. JSONMessageSerializer serializes a dataclass from Object.entries, not dataclasses.fields, and passes every decoded field to the constructor instead of splitting init and non-init fields. str()/repr() of None, floats and bools in classifier error messages and in OpenAI Decisions probability keys does not match Python exactly (for example 'None' vs '', 1.0 vs 1).

- `pipecat.observers.startup_timing_observer`: Dropped deprecated StartupWarmupTiming, StartupTimingObserver.on_startup_warmup and StartupTimingReport.warmup (with them the internal _warmup/_warmup_finished_ns; setup phase now ends at _setupFinishedNs, which is what upstream computes when warmup never fires). Pydantic models -> classes with options constructors.
- `pipecat.observers.turn_tracking_observer`: The deprecated max_frames parameter is dropped; the options are { turnEndTimeoutSecs }, and observeEveryPush is always false as upstream. The loop.call_later timer becomes a createTask (@pipedog/asyncio) that sleeps and then ends the turn. Once the sleep finishes it clears its own handle, so a later cancel no longer stops the callback, like a call_later handle that has fired. cleanup() cancels it. The only typecheck error is the shared TS6305 @pipedog/asyncio project-reference build issue.
- `pipecat.observers.user_bot_latency_observer`: Dropped the deprecated `maxFrames` option and `LatencyBreakdown.chronological_events()`. Pydantic models became classes with options constructors and camelCase fields. Private enums: _MomentKind is an as-const object; _Owner uses unique symbols so it can be told apart from literal string owners. The default timeSource is `time()` from @pipedog/asyncio. Typechecks clean.
- `pipecat.pipeline`: Empty upstream __init__.py; index.ts is `export {}`. | UNRESOLVED: Minor, left as is: Python's json.dumps escapes non-ASCII and JSON.stringify does not, so the wire bytes differ but parse to the same values. - `pipecat.pipeline.base_pipeline`: Broad catches call rethrowIfCancelled. Uses gather() and monotonicNs(). Typechecks clean.
- `pipecat.pipeline.capabilities`: BotCapabilities is a class with camelCase fields. Added modelDump({excludeNone}) and toJSON() that output upstream's snake_case keys, because RTVI sends this object to the client in bot-ready.
- `pipecat.pipeline.job_context`: Dropped the deprecated resolve_job_params(). `async with` became enter()/exit(error) plus a run(fn) helper; `async for` became Symbol.asyncIterator. The asyncio.shield around cancelJobGroup is kept. Assumes BaseWorker (workers/base_worker.ts, not written yet) exposes `jobGroups: Map<string, JobGroup>`, `createJobGroupAndRequestJob(names, {params})` and `cancelJobGroup(id, {reason})`. JobGroup.responses is a Record keyed by worker name. Typechecks clean except for the missing base_worker import.
- `pipecat.pipeline.job_decorator`: Follows the style of the sibling worker_ready_decorator.ts: job({name, sequential}) works as a TC39 decorator or a plain function, and _collectJobHandlers returns a Map of bound methods. The bound methods keep the isJobHandler/jobName/jobSequential flags.
- `pipecat.pipeline.llm_switcher`: Dropped deprecated register_direct_function. strategy_type is passed as options.strategyType. It calls each member's protected _syncRegisteredToolHandlers through a cast. runInference forwards the LLMService.runInference options object.
- `pipecat.pipeline.llm_with_backend`: Uses the TS _BackendSession API from workers/llm/backend_llm_worker.ts (run()/async iterator/send/detached) in place of async with. BackendRequestStrategy class attributes are instance fields. RuntimeError/ValueError/assert become Error.
- `pipecat.pipeline.parallel_pipeline`: Variadic constructor(...args: FrameProcessor[][]).
- `pipecat.pipeline.pipeline`: Constructor signatures: `new Pipeline(processors, {source, sink})`, `new PipelineSource(fn, options)`, `new PipelineSink(fn, options)`. The required positional argument stays positional. Typechecks clean.
- `pipecat.pipeline.runner`: Whole module is deprecated: it only re-exports WorkerRunner (import it from workers/runner.ts) and holds the deprecated PipelineRunner alias. No file written.
- `pipecat.pipeline.service_switcher`: Dropped the deprecated ErrorFrame.fatal check in push_frame (the field isn't ported). strategy_type -> options.strategyType. dataclasses.replace(frame) -> shallow copy + renewFrameIdentity (metadata/pts are carried over rather than reset). The deque(maxlen=64) is an array capped at 64. The isUsable getter is overridden.
- `pipecat.pipeline.sync_parallel_pipeline`: frame_order keyword -> an optional trailing options object after the processor lists. FrameOrder is an as-const enum.
- `pipecat.pipeline.task`: Whole module is deprecated: it re-exports from pipeline/worker.ts and includes the deprecated PipelineTask alias. Import from pipeline/worker.ts instead. No file written.
- `pipecat.pipeline.worker`: Tracing: `_enableTracing = enableTracing && isTracingAvailable()` as upstream, but rechecked in async _setup() after `await loadOpenTelemetry()` (core imports @opentelemetry/api asynchronously); if it became available, the tracing context and the latency/turn trace observers are created then (_createTracingObservers()) and added to the WorkerObserver before it starts, so a worker built at module top level still traces. Dropped (deprecated): PipelineTask, PipelineTaskParams, the toolResources option, the FrameProcessorSetup tool_resources field, the ErrorFrame.fatal branch, the StartFrame config fields (now `new StartFrame()`), and the legacy setup_pipeline_task hook name. Signature is `new PipelineWorker(pipeline, options)`. ProcessorUnusablePolicy is an as-const object; PipelineParams is a class. Calling `BaseWorker.cancel(self)` directly became `BaseWorker.prototype.cancel.call(this, ...)`. Assumed API of the unwritten workers/base_worker.ts: _finishedEvent, _children, bus, sendBusMessage, start/stop/end/cancel/activateWorker/onBusMessage, _handleWorkerEnd/_handleWorkerCancel, _propagate*ToChildren, _printDanglingTasks. rtvi/index.ts and rtvi/frames.ts are also not written yet. Against temporary stubs of those three modules (in a scratch copy, since deleted), the file typechecks with zero errors.
- `pipecat.pipeline.worker_observer`: Dropped deprecated on_startup_warmup / StartupWarmup handling. weakref.WeakValueDictionary of pushed frames is emulated with Map<id, WeakRef<Frame>> plus FinalizationRegistry (internal WeakFrameMap). The `Proxy` class keeps its upstream name and carries a biome-ignore because it shadows the global Proxy. The proxy tasks wait on asyncio Queue.get(), which is a cancellation point, so cancelTask() stops them cleanly. File: packages/core/src/pipeline/worker_observer.ts
- `pipecat.pipeline.worker_ready_decorator`: workerReady({ name }) returns a decorator that works both as a TC39 method decorator and as a plain call, e.g. `static { workerReady({name})(this.prototype.m) }`, because Node type-stripping does not run decorators. It follows the same pattern as the concurrently ported job_decorator.ts. _collectWorkerReadyHandlers walks the prototype chain and returns Map<string, boundFn> instead of a dict, and throws Error instead of ValueError.
- `pipecat.processors`: Empty upstream __init__.py; index.ts is `export {}`. | UNRESOLVED: Minor, left as is: Python's json.dumps escapes non-ASCII and JSON.stringify does not, so the wire bytes differ but parse to the same values. JSONMessageSerializer serializes a dataclass from Object.entries, not dataclasses.fields, and passes every decoded field to the constructor instead of splitting init and non-init fields. str()/repr() of None, floats and bools in classifier error messages and in OpenAI Decisions probability keys does not match Python exactly (for example 'None' vs '', 1.0 vs 1).
- `pipecat.processors.aggregators`: Empty __init__.py -> index.ts with header and export {}.
- `pipecat.processors.aggregators.async_tool_messages`: The payload JSON is built to match Python json.dumps(ensure_ascii=False) byte for byte (', ' and ': ' separators), so contexts are identical across ports. AsyncToolMessagePayload is a frozen class (Object.freeze) with camelCase fields; wire keys are unchanged. Use it as a module namespace: import * as asyncToolMessages.
- `pipecat.processors.aggregators.dtmf_aggregator`: Positional timeout/termination_digit/prefix -> options object (DTMFAggregatorOptions).
- `pipecat.processors.aggregators.gated`: Constructor keeps upstream's positional params (gateOpenFn, gateCloseFn, startOpen, direction) because upstream has no keyword-only args. Added a GateFn type alias.
- `pipecat.processors.aggregators.gated_llm_context`: Kept upstream's `if` (not `elif`) after the StartFrame branch, so a StartFrame is pushed twice exactly as upstream does. A comment marks it. Imports BaseNotifier from utils/sync/base_notifier.ts, which another agent is porting.
- `pipecat.processors.aggregators.llm_context`: LLMStandardMessage and LLMContextToolChoice are structural TS types; upstream aliases them to OpenAI types for type-checking only. LLMContext takes an options object ({messages, tools, toolChoice}). Static create*Message helpers take options objects. getMessages(filter, {truncateLargeValues}). The asyncio.to_thread audio encoding runs inline. Base64 uses a local btoa helper. Deep copies use structuredClone. setMessages replaces the array in place, like Python's slice assignment. customTools may be a Map or a record. Deps: utils/image.encodeImage is a stub upstream of this module and cannot JPEG-encode raw pixels yet, so createImageMessage only works with images that are already encoded.
- `pipecat.processors.aggregators.llm_context_summarizer`: uuid4 becomes crypto.randomUUID(). The dedicated LLM's protected `_generateSummary` is called through a cast, as upstream calls it directly. `template.format(summary=...)` becomes a small helper that replaces {summary} and un-doubles {{ and }}. setup() takes FrameProcessorSetup, typed `| unknown` to match the BaseObject.setup signature.
- `pipecat.processors.aggregators.llm_response_universal`: Dropped deprecated params fields: LLMUserAggregatorParams.filter_incomplete_user_turns and user_turn_completion_config (and the FilterIncompleteUserTurnStrategies translation that depended on them), and LLMAssistantAggregatorParams.enable_context_summarization and context_summarization_config (with the LLMContextSummarizationConfig.to_auto_config path). Dataclasses become classes with options constructors. Python's `__iter__` on the pair becomes [Symbol.iterator], so destructured values are typed as a union of the two aggregators. `_userTurnController`, `_realtimeHandoffFlush` and `_realtimeHandoffFlushImmediate` are public (underscore names kept) because the paired assistant aggregator uses them, and TS protected would block that. VADController._vadAnalyzer is read through a cast. json.dumps(ensure_ascii=False) becomes a local helper with Python's ', ' and ': ' separators; floats like 1.0 still print as 1. Python truthiness of frame.result is copied by an isTruthy helper (empty dicts and lists are falsy). RuntimeError becomes Error. Marker constants are imported statically from turns/user_turn_completion_mixin.ts instead of upstream's lazy import.
- `pipecat.processors.aggregators.llm_text_processor`: PatternMatch is imported from utils/text/pattern_pair_aggregator.ts and SimpleTextAggregator from utils/text/simple_text_aggregator.ts; other agents own both modules.
- `pipecat.processors.async_generator`: Python truthiness check on serialized data (empty string/bytes are skipped). Imports FrameSerializer from serializers/base_serializer.ts.
- `pipecat.processors.audio`: Empty __init__.py -> index.ts with header and export {}.
- `pipecat.processors.audio.audio_buffer_processor`: Dropped deprecated on_user_turn_audio_data / on_bot_turn_audio_data events (1.8.0) and the addEventHandler override that warned about them; their internal run-folding (_endUserRun/_endBotRun) is kept. bytearray buffers are an exported growable ByteBuffer helper.
- `pipecat.processors.audio.vad_processor`: Reads startSecs/stopSecs from the vadAnalyzer passed in (the same object as upstream's controller._vad_analyzer), so it does not depend on VADController internals. on_broadcast_frame kwargs arrive as one options object.
- `pipecat.processors.filters`: Empty __init__.py -> index.ts with header and export {}.
- `pipecat.processors.filters.frame_filter`: The positional `types` tuple is now the options object { types: FrameClass[] }.
- `pipecat.processors.filters.function_filter`: Positional filter/direction/filter_system_frames become the options object { filter, direction, filterSystemFrames }. direction is DOWNSTREAM when undefined, and an explicit null filters both directions.
- `pipecat.processors.filters.wake_check_filter`: The whole module is deprecated (WakeCheckFilter, since 0.0.106; replaced by WakePhraseUserTurnStartStrategy), so no TS file was written.
- `pipecat.processors.filters.wake_notifier_filter`: The positional notifier is now the options object { notifier, types, filter }.
- `pipecat.processors.frame_processor`: hand-ported reference
- `pipecat.processors.frameworks`: Empty __init__.py -> index.ts with header and export {}.
- `pipecat.processors.frameworks.langchain`: Ported to /Users/pol/vscode/pipedog/packages/langchain/src/langchain.ts. New package @pipedog/langchain (package.json, tsconfig.json); @langchain/core is a peer dependency (>=0.3.0) and a dev dependency pinned to 1.2.20. bun install ran, so bun.lock changed. Constructor is (chain, { transcriptKey = 'input' }). Python's chain.astream becomes Runnable.stream, which receives configurable.session_id and currentSignal(); each stream step is wrapped in abortable() so cancellation can interrupt it, and the catch starts with rethrowIfCancelled. Python's isinstance(dict) check becomes: a plain object that is not an LLMSpecificMessage. AIMessageChunk is detected with AIMessageChunk.isInstance. DIVERGENCE: list content is turned into text with JSON.stringify, not Python's str() repr. The GeneratorExit branch has no JS equivalent and is not ported; a comment marks where it was, and the iterator is closed in a finally block. tsc and biome report no errors in this file.
- `pipecat.processors.frameworks.rtvi`: index.ts re-exports frames, models (BotOutputTransformResult, SpokenProgressData), observer and processor, as upstream __all__ does.
- `pipecat.processors.frameworks.rtvi.frames`: All frames, including RTVIConfigureObserverFrame. RTVIFunctionCallReportLevel is imported type-only from observer.ts to avoid a circular import. In toString(), object values are JSON.stringify'd where Python prints a dict repr.
- `pipecat.processors.frameworks.rtvi.models`: Pydantic models become classes on a small internal BaseModel (no zod dependency). Fields are camelCase via `declare` plus a static field table. Constructors validate camelCase options; static modelValidate() validates wire snake_case data, recursively. modelDump({excludeNone}) and toJSON() emit the upstream snake_case keys. extra='allow' models (A11yNode, A11ySelection, A11ySnapshot) keep unknown keys in modelExtra. Exports a ValidationError (name 'ValidationError'), which processor.ts's isValidationError already recognizes. Additions with no upstream counterpart in this module: ValidationError, BaseModel, ModelDumpOptions, SignalMessageOptions / TextMessageOptions / AudioLevelMessageOptions helper types. Dropped (deprecated): LLMFunctionCallMessageData and LLMFunctionCallMessage, which only the deprecated RTVIProcessor.handle_function_call uses. Kept although deprecated, because the observer still sends them by default and clients read them: BotLLMTextMessage, BotTTSTextMessage, UserTranscriptionMessage/Data, and BotOutputMessageData.aggregatedBy together with its text_type sync validator. Divergence: validation is strict and does not do pydantic's lax coercions (for example the string '5' is not accepted as an int). Remaining tsc error at observer.ts:651 belongs to the observer port: it passes a `{}`-typed args value to LLMFunctionCallInProgressMessageData.arguments, which expects Record<string, unknown>.
- `pipecat.processors.frameworks.rtvi.observer`: Not ported because upstream deprecates them: RTVIObserverParams.userTranscriptionEnabled and skipAggregatorTypes, the aggregationType argument of add/removeBotOutputTransformer, and the 2-parameter (text, textType) transform signature. Every transform is now called with 4 arguments. With userTranscriptionEnabled gone, user-transcription is always sent, which is the upstream default. The deprecated wire messages bot-llm-text, bot-tts-text, bot-transcription and user-transcription are still sent because current clients consume them. models.ts is not written yet, so this assumes RTVI models are classes with camelCase constructor options and a modelDump({ excludeNone }) method that returns snake_case wire keys. metrics.ts has no modelDump, so a local helper dumps metrics to snake_case and drops nulls. Loguru's async sink becomes logger.addSink, which runs each send as a tracked createTask. Every broad catch calls rethrowIfCancelled. tsc: the only errors left come from missing rtvi/frames.ts and rtvi/models.ts (unresolved imports and the instanceof narrowing that depends on them). | UNRESOLVED: processor.ts base64ToBytes uses atob, which differs from Python's base64.b64decode at the edges: Python rejects missing padding and silently drops non-alphabet characters, while atob accepts missing padding and throws on non-alphabet characters. Exact parity would need a hand-written decoder. I left it as is. | UNRESOLVED: observer.ts _handleContext: upstream fails on a content-part list that holds a string item or a non-string text (TypeError, caught and logged), while the TS skips such items or converts them to strings. Base64 is decoded inline with atob (asyncio.to_thread is not needed). - `pipecat.processors.idle_frame_processor`: DIVERGENCE: cleanup() also calls super.cleanup() (upstream doesn't), so the base processor's input/process tasks are released.
- `pipecat.processors.logger`: constructor(prefix = "Frame", { color, ignoredFrameTypes }). Our logger has no loguru markup, so loguru color names (red, light-blue, bold...) are mapped to ANSI escapes and unknown names log uncolored.

- `pipecat.runner`: index.ts re-exports types.ts and utils.ts (upstream's __init__ is empty); the dev server is @pipedog/runner/run and /serve.
- `pipecat.runner.daily`: Needs the Daily transport (pipecat.transports.daily), not ported; -t daily / --direct fail clearly.
- `pipecat.runner.livekit`: generateToken, generateTokenWithAgent, livekitCredentials, generateSessionTokens, configure, configureWithArgs on livekit-server-sdk (pure JS). DIVERGENCE: token functions are async (WebCrypto); configureWithArgs takes an optional argv and uses ArgumentParser.parseKnownArgs (added to argparse.ts).
- `pipecat.runner.moq`: Needs the MOQ transport, not ported; -t moq fails clearly.
- `pipecat.runner.run`: The FastAPI app is RunnerApp, a router over a fetch handler (routes, websocket routes, mounts, lifespans, CORS); createRunnerApp() builds it from options, serve() (serve.ts) runs it on Bun/Node/Deno via transport-websocket's serveFetch. main() parses upstream's flags with a small ArgumentParser (argparse.ts), finds bot() on the entry script / bot.ts / cwd scripts, loads .env. Routes: /, /client (prebuilt.ts serves the pipecat-ai-prebuilt 1.3.0 build vendored in prebuilt/), /start, /status, /api/offer POST/PATCH and /sessions/{id}/... proxy (smallWebRTCOfferHandler), /ws(/{token}) telephony with XML webhook POST /, /ws-client(/{token}), /files uploads/downloads. DIVERGENCE: module globals (RUNNER_FILE_STORAGE, _SESSION_UPLOAD_URLS) live on the app; WS token helpers and _resolveDownloadPath are async; uploads storage resolved on first use; startup hooks are lifespan functions returning a cleanup. LiveKit: /start mints session tokens (runner/livekit.ts) and starts the bot with LiveKitRunnerArguments. Not ported: Daily routes/direct/dial-in, MOQ (flags too), Vonage, WhatsApp, eval, deprecated --folder and -t eval (they fail with a clear message).
- `pipecat.runner.types`: All runner argument classes and DialinSettings/DailyDialinRequest (fromDict/toDict for the wire keys). Deprecated EvalRunnerArguments and MOQ serve_bind not ported. WebSocketRunnerArguments adds pipedog's handshake (TelephonyHandshake). Daily/Vonage/MOQ classes are kept for API parity; their transports are not ported. CallData: dict-style access is getItem/get/has; modelValidate mimics pydantic.
- `pipecat.runner.utils`: createTransport, _getTransportParams, _createTelephonyTransport (serializers lazily imported), smallwebrtcSdpMunging, parseTelephonyWebsocket. eval-connect detection is ported (type "eval"): createTransport builds EvalFastAPIWebsocketTransport, importing the optional peer @pipedog/evals lazily; the deprecated EvalRunnerArguments/EvalTransport branch is not ported. DIVERGENCE: a hang-up before the provider's start message is TelephonyCallEndedError (extends TelephonyHandshakeError/ValueError) with runnerArgs.handshake set, for 0 or 1 messages (upstream: ValueError, or 'Missing transport params for unknown'). Not ported: _maybe_apply_daily_dialin and the Daily/Vonage/MOQ transports (TransportNotPortedError); LiveKit builds LiveKitTransport from @pipedog/transport-livekit (optional peer, imported by name), deprecated get_transport_client_id and maybe_capture_participant_*.
- `pipecat.runner.vonage`: Needs the Vonage Video Connector transport, not ported; -t vonage fails clearly.
- `pipecat.serializers`: Empty upstream __init__.py; index.ts is `export {}`. | UNRESOLVED: Minor, left as is: Python's json.dumps escapes non-ASCII and JSON.stringify does not, so the wire bytes differ but parse to the same values. JSONMessageSerializer serializes a dataclass from Object.entries, not dataclasses.fields, and passes every decoded field to the constructor instead of splitting init and non-init fields. str()/repr() of None, floats and bools in classifier error messages and in OpenAI Decisions probability keys does not match Python exactly (for example 'None' vs '', 1.0 vs 1).
- `pipecat.serializers.base_serializer`: The nested InputParams is the exported FrameSerializerInputParams, also reachable as the static FrameSerializer.InputParams. setup() takes FrameProcessorSetup | unknown to keep BaseObject.setup's signature. Added a SerializedData = string | Uint8Array type.
- `pipecat.serializers.exotel`: Ported in full. Golden vectors from upstream: byte-identical at 8 kHz, events with log lines.
- `pipecat.serializers.genesys`: Ported in full. The position (upstream timedelta) is kept as integer microseconds, formatted like f'{x:.3f}' (half-even on exact ties) and parsed like float()+timedelta. Session id uses crypto.randomUUID(). Upstream tests/genesys/test_genesys_serializer.py is not in the manifest (the tools can't add test entries); it is ported to packages/serializer-genesys/test/test_genesys_serializer.test.ts (27 pass), plus golden AudioHook sessions recorded from upstream.
- `pipecat.serializers.plivo`: Ported in full. Hang-up uses fetch in place of aiohttp. Golden vectors from upstream: byte-identical at 8 kHz, events and log lines, hang-up request and logs. No upstream test file; covered by test/golden.test.ts.
- `pipecat.serializers.protobuf`: New package @pipedog/serializer-protobuf (package.json, tsconfig.json). Its dependencies are @pipedog/core and @pipedog/asyncio (for rethrowIfCancelled). It uses core's hand-written codec, packages/core/src/frames/protobufs/frames_pb2.ts, so it needs no third-party protobuf library and runs on any runtime. Type-check is clean, biome is clean, and a quick round-trip smoke run worked (text, transcription, audio, interruption, transport message, and the warning for an unsupported type). Constructor: upstream's positional `params` is now an options object (`{ params }`). Matching is on the exact type, as upstream: SERIALIZABLE_TYPES and DESERIALIZABLE_TYPES are Maps keyed by constructor, so subclasses such as LLMTextFrame are not serializable, same as upstream. Serialize: it walks the proto submessage's fields (not dataclasses.fields) and copies a field when the frame has that property and the value is truthy in Python's sense (an empty Uint8Array counts as false). The submessage is attached to the oneof only once one of its fields is set, as with protobuf's getattr. Transport messages are JSON-encoded by a local copy of Python's json.dumps (', ' and ': ' separators, ensure_ascii escapes, TypeError for values that are not JSON), so MessageFrame.data matches upstream byte for byte. Core's pyJsonDumps in workers/ui/ui_worker.ts is not exported, so I could not reuse it. DIVERGENCE: deserialize(string) throws TypeError, the way Python protobuf's FromString rejects a str. A JSON.parse failure in a MessageFrame is caught after rethrowIfCancelled and logged. Not done: the test file test_protobuf_serializer.py is not ported, and runtimes in package.json is left empty for `bun run compat --write` to fill. | UNRESOLVED: JSON.parse in deserialize rejects NaN/Infinity/-Infinity, which Python's json.loads accepts. Messages that contain them make deserialize log an error and return null. The wording of the logged parse error also differs from Python's JSONDecodeError text. | UNRESOLVED: The 'Frame type X is not serializable' warning logs the constructor name. Python logs "<class 'pipecat.frames.frames.X'>" there.
- `pipecat.serializers.rtvi_client`: Ported in full to /Users/pol/vscode/pipedog/packages/serializer-rtvi-client/src/rtvi_client.ts. Upstream has nothing deprecated here, so nothing was dropped. I created the new package with package.json ("runtimes" left empty because `bun run compat --write` fills it) and a tsconfig.json that references ../core. The options type is Omit<FrameSerializerOptions, "params">, because upstream always forces params to InputParams(ignore_rtvi_messages=False). Output matches Python's json.dumps byte for byte through a local jsonDumps helper (", " and ": " separators, ensure_ascii escaping), and base64 goes through a local bytesToBase64 helper. Python dict.get, `or` and truthiness are copied with small local helpers. deserialize decodes bytes with TextDecoder and catches only SyntaxError/TypeError, like upstream's (JSONDecodeError, TypeError). Two small differences: (1) invalid UTF-8 bytes become replacement characters instead of raising UnicodeDecodeError; (2) a payload that is not a dict throws a TypeError, matching upstream's AttributeError on payload.get. The JS JSON parse error text in the warning log differs from Python's. tsc shows no errors for this file and biome passes. A throwaway smoke test (since deleted) produced the same raw-audio wire string as upstream and mapped user-transcription to InterimTranscriptionFrame correctly. The upstream tests are not ported yet. | UNRESOLVED: Log text for non-JSON input uses JavaScriptCore/V8's JSON.parse error message rather than Python's JSONDecodeError text (e.g. 'Expecting value: line 1 column 1 (char 0)'); not reproducible without a JSON parser port. | UNRESOLVED: jsonDumps cannot tell Python int from float (1.0 serializes as '1' rather than '1.0', -0.0 as '0', 1e21 as '1e+21' vs Python '1e+21'/exponent formats differ for some values); JS numbers carry no int/float distinction. Only matters for caller-built transport messages containing floats with integral values. | UNRESOLVED: Python json.loads(bytes) also auto-detects UTF-16/UTF-32 encodings; the port decodes bytes as UTF-8 only.
- `pipecat.serializers.telnyx`: Created the new package @pipedog/serializer-telnyx (package.json, tsconfig.json, src/telnyx.ts). The constructor takes an options object ({ streamId, outboundEncoding, inboundEncoding, callControlId, apiKey, params }) instead of positional args. The hang-up call uses fetch with currentSignal()/abortable. For the 422 response, the body is read once as text and then parsed as JSON, because a fetch body can only be consumed once (aiohttp caches it). JSON output goes through a local Python-json.dumps-compatible helper (', ' and ': ' separators, ensure_ascii), so the wire format matches upstream. Base64 uses btoa/atob, not Buffer. ValueError becomes Error. KeypadEntry(digit) validation checks the digit against the KeypadEntry values. Nothing upstream is deprecated, so nothing was dropped. tsc reports no errors. A manual smoke run of serialize/deserialize/DTMF and the credential check worked. Run `bun install` to link the new workspace package. pipecat.runtimes is left empty for `bun run compat --write` to fill.
- `pipecat.serializers.twilio`: Ported in full. Constructor takes an options object. Hang-up uses fetch (cancelled with the pipeline) in place of aiohttp; it reads the 404 body once and parses it, and keeps aiohttp's JSON content-type check. Basic credentials are UTF-8 encoded (aiohttp: Latin-1). Golden vectors from upstream (tools/golden/telephony.py): wire output byte-identical at 8 kHz, hang-up request and log lines identical; resampled audio compared by SNR (resampler-wasm vs libsoxr).
- `pipecat.serializers.vonage`: Ported in full. Golden vectors from upstream: binary audio byte-identical at 16 kHz, events with upstream's log lines (Python repr of dicts reproduced). No upstream test file; covered by test/golden.test.ts.

- `pipecat.services.anthropic`: Empty __init__; index.ts only has the header and `export {}`.
- `pipecat.services.anthropic.llm`: Port is in packages/anthropic/src/llm.ts. It uses @anthropic-ai/sdk, which I added to packages/anthropic/package.json as ^0.131.0 (released 2026-09-30) and installed with bun install, so bun.lock changed. Dropped as deprecated: AnthropicLLMService.InputParams and the model/params constructor arguments. Changes from upstream: (1) The JS SDK has no NOT_GIVEN, so temperature, topK, topP and thinking store null to mean 'not sent'. An explicit null sampling value is therefore omitted from the request, where upstream would send JSON null. Before sending, any request field that anthropicIsGiven rejects is dropped. (2) The JS SDK has no extra_body option, so params keep upstream's extra_body entry and it is merged into the request JSON when the request is sent. (3) The JS SDK has no RequestTooLargeError, so a 413 is matched by status. APIStatusError is matched as Anthropic.APIError with a status set. (4) With retryOnTimeout, the first attempt runs under waitFor with its own AbortController, and that request is aborted before the retry. Every SDK await goes through abortable() with currentSignal(). (5) tracedLlm is installed on _processContext in a static block. (6) AnthropicThinkingConfig gets modelDump({excludeUnset}) and fromDict in place of pydantic. (7) The _THINKING_DEFAULTS table and the _modelName and _applySamplingSettings helpers are exported for tests. One tsc error remains, at llm.ts:365 (TS2344): AnthropicLLMAdapter does not satisfy the constraint BaseLLMAdapter<Record<string, unknown>>. The cause is outside this file: core's LLMService<TAdapter extends BaseLLMAdapter> defaults to Record<string, unknown>, and the adapter's AnthropicLLMInvocationParams is an interface with no index signature. Fix it in core (constrain to BaseLLMAdapter<object>, as LLMAdapterClass already does) or make the adapter's params a type alias. Possible core bug: ServiceSettings.fromMapping builds a plain ServiceSettings instead of `this`. If so, the override that turns a thinking dict into AnthropicThinkingConfig would find thinking in extra and never run. Biome warns once about `this` in the static fromMapping; this is deliberate so subclasses work. | UNRESOLVED: I compared packages/anthropic/src/llm.ts against llm.py line by line and found no real divergence to fix. Defaults use ?? (or || only where Python's `or` is meant), the broad catch puts CancelledError first and calls rethrowIfCancelled before the generic branch, every await is kept, durations are in seconds, the token estimate uses \p{L}\p{N}_ and Math.trunc to match Python's \w and int(), usage fields use != null checks, the deprecated InputParams and model/params arguments are dropped, and the header and naming follow the conventions. | UNRESOLVED: Documented divergence I left as is: the sampling settings (temperature, topK, topP) and thinking default to null to stand in for Anthropic's NOT_GIVEN, so an explicitly set null is left out of the request where upstream would send JSON null. A cleaner fix would need the settings validation to accept ANTHROPIC_NOT_GIVEN (undefined) as a given value. | UNRESOLVED: Type error outside this file: tsc reports src/llm.ts(365,53) TS2344, 'AnthropicLLMAdapter' does not satisfy the constraint 'BaseLLMAdapter<Record<string, unknown>>'. It comes from the typing of AnthropicLLMInvocationParams in packages/anthropic/src/anthropic_adapter.ts (probably an interface with no index signature), not from llm.ts.

- `pipecat.services.aws`: Empty __init__.
- `pipecat.services.aws.agent_core`: InvokeAgentRuntime through a SigV4-signed fetch, with a UUID session-id header; all runtimes. DIVERGENCE: no default credential chain (credentialsProvider); SSE lines are buffered across body chunks and a single-part body is read whole.
- `pipecat.services.aws.llm`: Raw HTTP Converse/ConverseStream with SigV4 on WebCrypto, event stream decoded to botocore-shaped dicts. All runtimes. Deprecated InputParams and model/params/stop_sequences args not ported. DIVERGENCE: client_config not ported (no botocore retries or 300 s timeouts); no default credential chain (credentialsProvider instead).
- `pipecat.services.aws.nova_sonic`: Empty __init__.
- `pipecat.services.aws.nova_sonic.llm`: DIVERGENCE: InvokeModelWithBidirectionalStream goes over Bedrock's WebSocket endpoint by default (SigV4 presign plus chained event signing, byte-identical to @aws-sdk/middleware-websocket), so it runs on Bun/Node/Deno/Workers; transport 'http2' uses the optional peer @aws-sdk/client-bedrock-runtime (Node/Bun). Credentials are optional, falling back to env and then credentialsProvider. ready.wav is embedded as base64. Not ported (deprecated): Params, and the model/voice_id/params/system_instruction init args. InvalidStateError handling dropped.
- `pipecat.services.aws.nova_sonic.session_continuation`: Streams are a BidirectionalStream interface (closeInput = input_stream.close); createTask/cancelTask take functions.

- `pipecat.services.aws.sagemaker.bidi_client`: HTTP/2 duplex through the optional peer @aws-sdk/client-sagemaker-runtime-http2, imported lazily (Node, Bun; not Workers). Injectable SageMakerRuntimeHTTP2ClientLike client. JS SDK event shapes. Credentials come from explicit keys, env or credentialsProvider instead of EnvironmentCredentialsResolver. The classifier also reads the JS SDK's metadata, name and retryable fields.
- `pipecat.services.aws.stt`: Transcribe WebSocket API with a SigV4 presigned URL (WebCrypto); all runtimes. DIVERGENCE: no default credential chain (options/env/credentialsProvider); only the Origin handshake header is sent. endpointUrl is a pipedog addition. Deprecated language option not ported.
- `pipecat.services.aws.tts`: Polly REST /v1/speech through a SigV4-signed fetch; all runtimes. DIVERGENCE: no default credential chain (credentialsProvider); every request failure becomes an ErrorFrame. Deprecated InputParams/voice_id/params not ported.
- `pipecat.services.aws.utils`: SigV4 on WebCrypto (sigv4.ts) and a full event-stream codec with CRC32 (event_stream.ts); getPresignedUrl/getRequestUrl are async and take an optional date. DIVERGENCE: no botocore default credential chain; services take a credentialsProvider (aws_session.ts) instead. decodeEvent throws EventStreamError instead of AssertionError.
- `pipecat.services.azure`: Empty __init__.
- `pipecat.services.azure.common`: packages/azure/src/common.ts. AzureTokenProvider is () => Promise<string>.
- `pipecat.services.azure.image`: packages/azure/src/image.ts. DIVERGENCE: fetch (option) replaces the required aiohttp_session; PNG decoded with decodePngToRgb (decodeImage option) and the frame format is always RGB (upstream: Pillow's mode). Deprecated image_size and model init params not ported.
- `pipecat.services.azure.llm`: packages/azure/src/llm.ts. v1 surface uses the openai SDK's OpenAI client, the dated surface AzureOpenAI. createClient() reads endpoint/tokenProvider from the constructor options (it runs inside the parent constructor). With a token provider the key is passed as null (the JS client rejects both). Deprecated model and api_version init params not ported.
- `pipecat.services.azure.realtime`: Empty __init__.
- `pipecat.services.azure.realtime.llm`: packages/azure/src/realtime/llm.ts. Socket via core websocketConnect with api-key / Authorization: Bearer headers.
- `pipecat.services.azure.stt`: packages/azure/src/stt.ts. DIVERGENCE: no Speech SDK; talks the Speech WebSocket protocol (/speech/recognition/conversation/cognitiveservices/v1, format=simple) directly, with profanity, segmentation silence timeout, language and cid as query params like the JS SDK. _speechConfig is an AzureSpeechConfig stand-in with setProfanity/setProperty. Connects in start() (sample rate known), reconnects on audio after a drop; non-1000 close reported via _onHandleCanceled; callbacks are awaited from the receive task. Key sent as Ocp-Apim-Subscription-Key header and query param. Deprecated language init param not ported.
- `pipecat.services.azure.tts`: packages/azure/src/tts.ts. DIVERGENCE: no Speech SDK. AzureTTSService speaks the synthesis WebSocket protocol (tts/cognitiveservices/websocket/v1: speech.config, synthesis.context with word boundaries, ssml; audio, audio.metadata, turn.end); audio duration computed from PCM received; interruption closes the socket (upstream stop_speaking_async). AzureHttpTTSService uses REST /cognitiveservices/v1 with raw-*-16bit-mono-pcm, so no 44-byte WAV strip. Handlers are synchronous with putNowait. Deprecated voice, params/InputParams, aggregate_sentences not ported.

New option: webSocketFactory, to open the socket some other way (Workers need none: core opens it through fetch there). Messages are plain parsed JSON, not SDK models. Float query params use JS String(), so 1.0 becomes "1" where Python writes "1.0". The session_started debug log prints the data dict as JSON rather than as a Python repr. Every broad catch calls rethrowIfCancelled first. Defaults use ?? except where upstream's `or` is meant to coalesce falsy values (flushAudio's context id, elevenlabsLanguageCode's model). - `pipecat.services.google.gemini_live.llm`: Full port in packages/google/src/gemini_live/llm.ts on @google/genai Live (callback session feeding an asyncio Queue, the same pattern as gemini_live/stt.ts). Deprecated items left out: the InputParams class and the model/voiceId/params constructor args. Tracing is installed in a static block (tracedGeminiLive on _toolResult, _handleSessionReady, _handleMsgToolCall, _handleMsgTurnComplete; installTracedStt on _handleUserTranscription). Divergences: (1) historyConfig is set on the config, but @google/genai 2.27 does not send it in the setup message. (2) The interaction_status SDK-version warning never fires, because the JS SDK passes Gemini API messages through unchanged; it is kept behind the static _SDK_SUPPORTS_INTERACTION_STATUS = true flag. (3) Video goes through core encodeImage, so an already-encoded image (format is a MIME type) is sent as is, where upstream's Pillow call would fail. (4) A plain string that happens to be a Language value (e.g. "es") is converted to the Gemini locale, where upstream would send it as given. Null generation-config fields are dropped before sending (pydantic does the same). Its callbacks (onmessage/onerror/onclose) feed an asyncio Queue that the receive task reads, standing in for session.receive(); socket errors and closes are raised in the receive loop, so they go through upstream's reconnect / MAX_CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES path. __aenter__/__aexit__ map to connect()/session.close(); the close cause is only logged. The google-genai>=2.9.0 ImportError check is dropped (no runtime types in JS); the transcription config (languageHints/languageAuto/adaptationPhrases/mode) is sent as plain camelCase objects, which 2.8.0 typings lack, hence a cast. Audio is sent base64. The ttfsP99Latency default is GEMINI_TTFS_P99. Imports languageToGoogleSttLanguage from ../stt.ts (another agent's module; it already exists). - `pipecat.services.google.stt`: Ported to /Users/pol/vscode/pipedog/packages/google/src/stt.ts. Contains languageToGoogleSttLanguage (the full language map), _modelSupportsAdaptation, _normalizeSpeechAdaptation, GoogleSTTSettings and GoogleSTTService. Ported deprecated-code handling, request generator, stream loop with streaming-limit reconnects, ABORTED handling, response processing, usage metrics, and tracing via installTracedStt. Speech V2 streaming is gRPC-only, so the default client is the Node SDK @google-cloud/speech (v2 SpeechClient._streamingRecognize). It is an optional peer that is imported lazily through a variable specifier, so importing the module never loads it. A `client` option takes any GoogleSTTClient (a streaming call that can be written to and read from, plus an optional getProjectId) for other transports or test fakes. The request generator is fed into the call by a tracked task (_sendRequests), and reading responses goes through abortable(). Every broad catch calls rethrowIfCancelled. Deprecated APIs dropped: InputParams and the `params` option, Settings.language_codes (so _getLanguageCodes uses only `languages`, then falls back to en-US), set_languages(), update_options(), and the language_codes deprecation warning in _updateSettings. DIVERGENCE: with credentialsPath or default credentials, the client and project ID are resolved on first connect (in setup()) instead of the constructor, so the 'No valid credentials provided.' and 'Project ID not found in credentials' errors appear there. Inline `credentials` JSON is still checked in the constructor. Protobuf SpeechAdaptation and DenoiserConfig messages are plain objects; snake_case or camelCase keys are accepted and converted to the SDK's camelCase field names when the request is built. Aborted (409) is detected as gRPC code 10. tsc and biome report no errors in this file. A quick run built the service and its settings, normalized an adaptation payload and printed the frame; it did not open a stream. | UNRESOLVED: packages/google/src/stt.ts: upstream _normalize_speech_adaptation returns a native SpeechAdaptation message unchanged (identity, test_normalize_speech_adaptation_accepts_native_message). In this port messages are plain objects, so the function always returns a normalized copy. A ported test should assert toEqual, not identity. | UNRESOLVED: packages/google/src/stt.ts: when phrase_sets is explicitly None, upstream raises a TypeError (list(None)). The port treats null as an empty list. This is an edge case and was left as is. | UNRESOLVED: packages/google/src/stt.ts: the porter's documented DIVERGENCE stays. With credentialsPath or ADC, credential and project ID errors are raised on first connect (in _ensureClient during setup), not in the constructor.
- `pipecat.services.google.tts`: Cloud TTS (GoogleHttpTTSService, GoogleTTSService, the GCP backend of GeminiTTSService) runs through @google-cloud/text-to-speech, added as an optional peer. It is loaded lazily with import() behind a small GoogleCloudTTSClient interface (createGoogleCloudTTSClient), so it needs Node/Bun; the GenAI backend uses @google/genai (added as a dependency) and stays portable. Divergences: with no credentials or credentialsPath, the SDK resolves ADC on the first request, so upstream's construction-time ValueError('No valid credentials provided.') shows up as a synthesis error instead. The SpeechMetadata SDK-version gate in _checkGenaiModelSupported never fires (GENAI_SUPPORTS_SPEECH_METADATA=true). _closeClient only calls aclose() if the client has one, because the JS GenAI client has none. Base64 inlineData is decoded. multi-speaker speaker_configs keep snake_case keys (speaker_alias/speaker_id). Tracing goes through installTracedTts in static blocks. Dropped as deprecated: the InputParams classes, the params/voiceId/(Gemini) model constructor options, the GoogleStreamTTSSettings alias and _warnInitParamMovedToSettings calls. GRPC_ENABLE_FORK_SUPPORT is not ported. Typecheck is clean against @google/genai 2.8.0 types (checked through a temporary link that was then removed). The only remaining tsc error is TS2307 'Cannot find module @google/genai', because the package is not installed yet. - `pipecat.services.llm_service`: packages/core/src/services/llm_service.ts. LLMService<TAdapter> extends UserTurnCompletionLLMServiceMixin(AIService); WebsocketLLMService<TAdapter> extends WebsocketService(LLMService)<TAdapter>. Deprecated APIs left out: FunctionCallParams.tool_resources, register_direct_function, unregister_direct_function (and its internal _unregister_direct_function, which nothing else used), the enable_async_tool_cancellation option (so _isCancellable checks only cancellableByLlm), _warn_turn_completion_settings_are_strategy_owned, and the legacy LLMUpdateSettingsFrame(settings={...}) dict path. The internal _registerDirectFunction is kept because auto-registration uses it. New in TS: an exported NotImplementedError class (Python's builtin), since runInference throws it and _generateSummary catches it. Python bound methods compare equal; JS closures don't, so the done callback, the missing-function handler and the cancel-tool handler are stored once as # fields so identity checks still match. On cancellation: when a call is cancelled (interruption, timeout or the LLM's cancel tool), its handler's task is cancelled through @pipedog/asyncio. The JSDoc tells handlers to pass currentSignal() to fetch and other foreign awaits, or wrap them in abortable(), since those awaits cannot be stopped from outside otherwise. The toolOptions attributes on handlers are read in camelCase (_pipecatCancelOnInterruption, _pipecatTimeoutSecs, _pipecatCancellableByLlm, _pipecatCleanup); log lines show the snake_case option names. Small differences: log lines use JSON.stringify where Python uses repr or json.dumps (different spacing), and a ConnectionError is a plain Error. Python 'or' semantics kept where it matters (a 0 timeout falls back to the default). Typecheck is clean and a smoke test (construct, registerFunction, system-instruction composition) passed. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/services/llm_service.ts: `bunx biome check` reports formatting differences (for example the `_cancelFunctionCall` call layout around line 2485). They are layout only, not behavior, and the task said not to restyle correct code, so I left them. Running `bunx biome format --write` on the file would fix them. | UNRESOLVED: Not runtime-checked: `bun run typecheck` passes, but packages/core has no test directory, so none of these four modules were exercised by tests. | UNRESOLVED: Reviewed and left as-is (correct ports or allowed omissions): settings.ts and tests/utils.ts match upstream with no real divergences. websocket_service.ts matches too: every broad catch calls rethrowIfCancelled, CancelledError is rethrown first in `_receiveTaskHandler`, `??` defaults and durations in seconds are kept, and the backoff argument order is right. In llm_service.ts, `||` is used on purpose in two places to copy Python `or`: the summarization timeout and the per-tool vs global function-call timeout. The deprecated APIs were correctly left out and are listed in the file header: register/unregister_direct_function, tool_resources, enable_async_tool_cancellation, the turn-completion warnings and the legacy settings dict path. The filterIncompleteUserTurns and userTurnCompletionConfig settings fields are kept because the user turn strategy still uses them. | UNRESOLVED: On the side agent's note about stopping in-progress work on interruption: this port stops an interrupted function call by cancelling the task running it, using @pipedog/asyncio. That only takes effect when the handler reaches an await on a cancellable primitive. A handler stuck on an outside await (fetch, an SDK, a WebSocket) keeps running unless it is passed `currentSignal()` or wrapped in `abortable()`. The header of llm_service.ts says this, but the rule is not enforced in code. This review found no step that misses the stop signal; whether to use Effect for the core is a separate design decision.

Ports OpenAILiveLLMSettings, ResponsesDelegation, ClientDelegation, _TurnGrouper, _LiveDelegation, _PendingResponse and OpenAILiveLLMService, along with the chunking and token-estimate helpers. The helpers work on code points, as Python does. The WebSocket is opened with core's websocketConnect and an Authorization header. The header is sent on Bun, Node, Deno and Cloudflare Workers (where core opens the socket through fetch with an Upgrade header). A webSocketFactory option was added that upstream does not have, for other runtimes or a custom socket. DIVERGENCE: upstream skips only ValueError from parse_server_event; this port skips any non-cancellation error from parseServerEvent, which is the only error that function raises. OpenAIResponsesReasoningConfig is sent as its non-null effort, summary and mode fields, which matches model_dump(exclude_none=True). The tools snapshot uses pyJsonDumps on a key-sorted copy, because pyJsonDumps has no sort_keys. The LLMService generic is OpenAILiveLLMAdapter & BaseLLMAdapter, the same pattern google uses. No deprecated APIs upstream.

- `pipecat.services.openai.realtime.events`: packages/openai/src/realtime/events.ts. The pydantic models become classes on a small local BaseModel. Fields are camelCase and each constructor takes an options object. modelValidate() reads snake_case wire keys, and modelDump({excludeNone}) / toJSON() write them, so the wire format is unchanged. modelCopy({update}) covers model_copy. SessionProperties.modelFields covers model_fields, mapping camelCase names to wire keys. TokenDetails keeps extra keys, available through modelExtra. Unions are resolved by type: the audio format, and turn detection as bool or config. The SessionProperties tools validator runs in the constructor through LLMContext._normalizeAndValidateTools. The SessionUpdateEvent override that turns turn_detection false into null is kept. Validation is reduced to required fields and the type literal of server events, which raise ValidationError. Value types are not checked. parseServerEvent also accepts UTF-8 bytes. A smoke test at runtime confirmed parse, dump, extra fields and the error message match upstream. No deprecated APIs upstream. No tsc errors.

Not ported because deprecated: the `model` and `sessionProperties` constructor options (use `settings`) and the `_warn_init_param_moved_to_settings` calls that went with them. Divergences: the websocket is core `websocketConnect()`; video frames are encoded with core `encodeImage()` instead of PIL, and the data URI uses the MIME type that returns; the tracing decorators (tracedOpenaiRealtime x3, tracedStt via installTracedStt) are applied in a static block; `get_args(ImageDetail)` is a local constant list; `fromMapping` reads `SessionProperties.modelFields` and accepts both snake_case and camelCase keys; `_handleEvtError` logs the event with JSON.stringify where Python uses the pydantic repr. The service type parameter is `OpenAIRealtimeLLMAdapter & BaseLLMAdapter`, because the adapter's invocation-params interface does not satisfy LLMService's `Record<string, unknown>` constraint. Audio resampling uses core `createStreamResampler()`, which is the soxr stub, so input audio that is not already 24 kHz throws until core has a resampler. CORE BUG FOUND, not fixed because it is outside this unit: `LLMService._resolveAdapterClass` in packages/core/src/services/llm_service.ts reads `LLMService.adapterClass` where it should read `this.adapterClass`. Every subclass's `static adapterClass` is therefore ignored, and in a smoke run both this service and the Responses services got OpenAILLMAdapter.
