- `pipecat.services.openai.responses.llm`: packages/openai/src/responses/llm.ts. Upstream `_BaseOpenAIResponsesLLMService` is used as a base of both LLMService (HTTP) and WebsocketLLMService (WS). It is ported as a defineMixin, `BaseOpenAIResponsesLLMServiceMixin`, with `_BaseOpenAIResponsesLLMService` as its plain-class form over LLMService. `OpenAIResponsesLLMService extends Mixin(WebsocketLLMService)`, and addVirtualBase makes `instanceof _BaseOpenAIResponsesLLMService` true for both services. The mixin's members are public (listed in the BaseOpenAIResponsesLLMServiceMembers and BaseOpenAIResponsesLLMServiceStatics interfaces). The HTTP client is the `openai` JS SDK, using the dependency another agent already added. Streamed events are plain objects, so upstream's isinstance checks became `event.type` checks. Each `next()` is wrapped in abortable(), and in `finally` the iterator is closed and the controller aborted, as base_llm does. APITimeoutError becomes APIConnectionTimeoutError, TIMEOUT_EXCEPTIONS becomes isTimeoutException(). The DefaultAsyncHttpxClient connection limits are dropped. OPENAI_NOT_GIVEN (undefined) is written straight into temperature, topP and maxCompletionTokens after the defaults are built, the same way base_llm does it. `_hashInputItems` uses WebCrypto SHA-256 over sort-keys JSON, which makes it, `_applyPreviousResponseOptimization` and `_storePreviousResponseState` async. The API key falls back to core getEnv('OPENAI_API_KEY'). tracedLlm is applied to both `_processContext` methods in static blocks. The adapter type is `OpenAIResponsesLLMAdapter & BaseLLMAdapter` (same constraint issue as realtime). Private names are exported for test ports: _RetryableError and its subclasses, _isOSeries, _THINKING_DEFAULTS, _modelName, _modelSupportsReasoning. Runtime is affected by the core `_resolveAdapterClass` bug described in the realtime entry: the adapter resolves to OpenAILLMAdapter until core is fixed. A cancelled TTFB timeout task re-raises CancelledError (per CONVENTIONS) where upstream swallows it. The keepalive assert becomes a thrown Error.

- `pipecat.services.websocket_service`: packages/core/src/services/websocket_service.ts. WebsocketService is a defineMixin mixin, and its members are public because declaration emit rejects private or protected members in class expressions. Upstream's websockets library has no JS equivalent, so this file adds WebsocketClientConnection over the global WebSocket (send, recv, async iteration, ping, close with closeTimeout, state), plus websocketConnect(uri, {closeTimeout, openTimeout, additionalHeaders, subprotocols, webSocketFactory}), the State constants, and the ConnectionClosed, ConnectionClosedOK and ConnectionClosedError errors. recv() and the opening handshake are cancellation points. _BoundedCloseConnection's log for an unacknowledged close is built into close(). Divergences: ping() only checks the socket is open, because the WebSocket API cannot send ping frames. Headers work with the Bun, Node (undici) and Deno WebSocket; on Cloudflare Workers (detected by WebSocketPair) the socket is opened through fetch with an Upgrade header, which sends them. webSocketFactory replaces the socket creation on other runtimes. ReportErrorCallback takes { forceTreatAsPermanent } as an options object, matching pushErrorFrame. exponentialBackoffTime is called with positional arguments, which is the signature the ported utils/network.ts has. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/services/llm_service.ts: `bunx biome check` reports formatting differences (for example the `_cancelFunctionCall` call layout around line 2485). They are layout only, not behavior, and the task said not to restyle correct code, so I left them. Running `bunx biome format --write` on the file would fix them. | UNRESOLVED: Not runtime-checked: `bun run typecheck` passes, but packages/core has no test directory, so none of these four modules were exercised by tests. | UNRESOLVED: Reviewed and left as-is (correct ports or allowed omissions): settings.ts and tests/utils.ts match upstream with no real divergences. websocket_service.ts matches too: every broad catch calls rethrowIfCancelled, CancelledError is rethrown first in `_receiveTaskHandler`, `??` defaults and durations in seconds are kept, and the backoff argument order is right. In llm_service.ts, `||` is used on purpose in two places to copy Python `or`: the summarization timeout and the per-tool vs global function-call timeout. The deprecated APIs were correctly left out and are listed in the file header: register/unregister_direct_function, tool_resources, enable_async_tool_cancellation, the turn-completion warnings and the legacy settings dict path. The filterIncompleteUserTurns and userTurnCompletionConfig settings fields are kept because the user turn strategy still uses them. | UNRESOLVED: On the side agent's note about stopping in-progress work on interruption: this port stops an interrupted function call by cancelling the task running it, using @pipedog/asyncio. That only takes effect when the handler reaches an await on a cancellable primitive. A handler stuck on an outside await (fetch, an SDK, a WebSocket) keeps running unless it is passed `currentSignal()` or wrapped in `abortable()`. The header of llm_service.ts says this, but the rule is not enforced in code. This review found no step that misses the stop signal; whether to use Effect for the core is a separate design decision.

Added isLanguage() helper to replace Python's Language(value) ValueError check in resolveLanguage. languageMap typed Partial<Record<Language,string>>. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/transports/base_output.ts MediaSender._resizeImage (around line 1008) is a stub that throws "not ported yet: image resizing (PIL Image.resize)". Upstream's _draw_image resizes with PIL (Image.frombytes(...).resize(desired_size), which defaults to BICUBIC and premultiplies alpha for RGBA). So in TS, any OutputImageRawFrame with a format whose size differs from videoOutWidth x videoOutHeight makes the video task throw, where upstream resizes it. I did not write a substitute resampler: PIL is not installed here, so its output could not be checked pixel for pixel, and RGBA premultiply rounding depends on the Pillow version. This needs an owner decision: either a pure-JS bicubic resampler copied from Pillow's Resample.c (22-bit fixed-point coefficients), or a resizer the transport injects.

- `pipecat.transports.smallwebrtc.connection`: aiortc -> werift 0.25.0 through rtc.ts (aiortc-shaped adapter: RTCPeerConnection events, transceivers, receiver._enabled, RemoteStreamTrack frames, sender replaceTrack pumps); Opus via libopus-wasm behind OpusCodec (opus.ts). SCTP clamp: werift sctp.mtu set so DATA chunk payload = PIPECAT_SCTP_MAX_CHUNK_SIZE (default 1100). TrackStatusMessage validated by hand (no pydantic). IceServer = RTCIceServer class. Options object adds videoDecoderFactory/videoEncoderFactory/opusCodec/werift. SmallWebRTCTrack.setAudioOutputFormat lets Opus decode straight to the pipeline rate. mDNS (.local) candidates are added in the background (werift resolves them in ~1 s) so PATCH returns at once; end-of-candidates waits behind them. werift reports 'disconnected', which aiortc never does; forwarded as upstream would.
- `pipecat.transports.smallwebrtc.request_handler`: HTTPException is a local class (FastAPI's). Constructor is an options object; adds connectionOptions (passed to each SmallWebRTCConnection). SmallWebRTCRequest/SmallWebRTCPatchRequest gain fromDict() for JSON bodies. ESP32 munging uses a local copy of runner.utils.smallwebrtc_sdp_munging (smallwebrtcSdpMunging) until @pipedog/runner exists. http.ts (ours) serves the runner's POST/PATCH /api/offer contract as Request->Response, plus a Node http adapter.
- `pipecat.transports.smallwebrtc.transport`: Dropped deprecated: capture_participant_video without participant_id / with a source as first arg (1.13.0). PyAV AudioFrame/VideoFrame/AudioResampler and aiortc tracks -> media.ts; cv2 conversions -> video.ts convertToRgb. DIVERGENCE: video codecs are injected (videoDecoderFactory/videoEncoderFactory); without a decoder no UserImageRawFrames are produced (warned once); RawVideoTrack needs an encoder. DIVERGENCE: RawAudioTrack.stop() settles queued writes and the client stops the old output track on disconnected/closed, so restart_pc doesn't hang the output transport until its write timeout. SmallWebRTC cases of tests/test_video_in_sources.py ported in packages/transport-smallwebrtc/test/test_video_in_sources_smallwebrtc.test.ts (15 pass, 1 skip: deprecated).

- `pipecat.transports.websocket.client`: Runs on the new runtime-agnostic socket layer in packages/transport-websocket/src/socket.ts (WebsocketConnection, websocketConnect over the global WebSocket). That file is pipedog's own, with no upstream counterpart. additionalHeaders is passed as { headers }, which works on Bun, Node (undici) and Deno; on Workers the socket is opened through fetch with an Upgrade header, which sends them too. Browsers can't send them. WebsocketClientCallbacks became a class with an options constructor. acquires/releases('connection') are applied in a static block. Divergence: WebsocketClientSession has a replaceable `_connect` field (defaults to websocketConnect), standing in for the upstream tests' monkeypatch of `websocket_connect`. The default serializer is imported from @pipedog/serializer-protobuf/protobuf. The connection's async iterator ends normally on close codes 1000, 1001 and 1005 and throws ConnectionClosedError on any other code, matching `websockets`. tsc is clean. | UNRESOLVED: No real divergences found, so nothing was edited. I compared client.ts, fastapi.ts, rtvi_client.ts and server.ts line by line against their Python sources and checked: event registrations and handler arguments; defaults (all use ??); truthiness (payload.length > 0 for bytes and str, allowedOrigins.length > 0, sessionTimeout and fixedAudioPacketSize use JS truthiness that matches Python for 0 and null); rethrowIfCancelled in every broad catch; the deliberate CancelledError re-raise in _monitorWebsocket; durations in seconds; Uint8Array buffer math in the fixed-size audio packetizer; the acquires/releases wrapping of connect/disconnect; and the deprecated WebsocketServer* aliases, which were correctly left out. | UNRESOLVED: Minor cosmetic difference, left as is: server.ts logs remoteAddress as 'host:port' (built by serve.ts), while upstream logs websockets' remote_address tuple, e.g. "('127.0.0.1', 12345)". | UNRESOLVED: Minor edge case, left as is: in rtvi_client.ts, _isBotReady decodes binary messages with a lenient TextDecoder, so invalid UTF-8 returns false. Upstream json.loads would raise an uncaught UnicodeDecodeError, and it also auto-detects UTF-16/32 input.
- `pipecat.transports.websocket.fastapi`: Bring your own socket: the transport takes a `FastAPIWebSocket` interface, which is the subset of Starlette's WebSocket the transport uses (headers.get, clientState, applicationState, receive() returning ASGI-style messages, sendBytes, sendText, close). WebsocketConnection implements it, and WebsocketConnection.fromWebSocket(ws, { headers }) wraps a WebSocketPair, Deno, ws or Hono socket. This keeps the upstream mock-based tests portable 1:1. Starlette's WebSocketState is re-exported as an as-const object. The origin check throws Error rather than ValueError. input_name/output_name moved into a trailing options object. The close task is a raw asyncio createTask with a done callback, as upstream does. The bytearray buffer became a growable Uint8Array. tsc is clean. | UNRESOLVED: No real divergences found, so nothing was edited. JSONMessageSerializer serializes a dataclass from Object.entries, not dataclasses.fields, and passes every decoded field to the constructor instead of splitting init and non-init fields. str()/repr() of None, floats and bools in classifier error messages and in OpenAI Decisions probability keys does not match Python exactly (for example 'None' vs '', 1.0 vs 1).

- `pipecat.turns.speculation_gate`: SpeculationState is an as-const enum. GatedFrame is the tuple [Frame, FrameDirection]. Constructor takes {name}. Members are protected _camelCase. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/transports/base_output.ts MediaSender._resizeImage (around line 1008) is a stub that throws "not ported yet: image resizing (PIL Image.resize)". Upstream's _draw_image resizes with PIL (Image.frombytes(...).resize(desired_size), which defaults to BICUBIC and premultiplies alpha for RGBA). So in TS, any OutputImageRawFrame with a format whose size differs from videoOutWidth x videoOutHeight makes the video task throw, where upstream resizes it. I did not write a substitute resampler: PIL is not installed here, so its output could not be checked pixel for pixel, and RGBA premultiply rounding depends on the Pillow version. This needs an owner decision: either a pure-JS bicubic resampler copied from Pillow's Resample.c (22-bit fixed-point coefficients), or a resizer the transport injects.

So in TS, any OutputImageRawFrame with a format whose size differs from videoOutWidth x videoOutHeight makes the video task throw, where upstream resizes it. I did not write a substitute resampler: PIL is not installed here, so its output could not be checked pixel for pixel, and RGBA premultiply rounding depends on the Pillow version. This needs an owner decision: either a pure-JS bicubic resampler copied from Pillow's Resample.c (22-bit fixed-point coefficients), or a resizer the transport injects.

- `pipecat.turns.user_turn_completion_mixin`: UserTurnCompletionLLMServiceMixin is a defineMixin class factory: LLMService becomes `extends UserTurnCompletionLLMServiceMixin(AIService)`. The base is cast to Ctor<FrameProcessor> internally because defineMixin requires `<B extends Ctor>`. Its _-prefixed members are public on the mixin interface (they are protected upstream). TurnMarker and IncompleteType are as-const enums. UserTurnCompletionConfig is a class with getters (markers, completionInstructions, shortPrompt, longPrompt). The prompt render functions are module-private, as upstream. CancelledError is swallowed in _incompleteTimeoutHandler, as upstream. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/transports/base_output.ts MediaSender._resizeImage (around line 1008) is a stub that throws "not ported yet: image resizing (PIL Image.resize)". Upstream's _draw_image resizes with PIL (Image.frombytes(...).resize(desired_size), which defaults to BICUBIC and premultiplies alpha for RGBA). So in TS, any OutputImageRawFrame with a format whose size differs from videoOutWidth x videoOutHeight makes the video task throw, where upstream resizes it. I did not write a substitute resampler: PIL is not installed here, so its output could not be checked pixel for pixel, and RGBA premultiply rounding depends on the Pillow version. This needs an owner decision: either a pure-JS bicubic resampler copied from Pillow's Resample.c (22-bit fixed-point coefficients), or a resizer the transport injects.
- `pipecat.turns.user_turn_controller`: Strategy event handlers are arrow-function fields, so the same reference is added in _setupStrategies and removed in _cleanupStrategies (Python bound methods compare equal). on_broadcast_frame forwards (frameCls, options). | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/services/tts_service.ts _transformText: upstream PronunciationTransform is a class with an async `__call__`, called directly as `await transform(text, text_type)`. The TS port calls each textTransforms entry as a function and checks `instanceof PronunciationTransform`, and `TextTransformFunction` does not include PronunciationTransform. utils/text/transforms/pronunciations.ts is not ported yet. If it is ported as a plain class with a method (not a callable function object), the call fails and the types reject passing `pronunciationTransformIpa()` output in textTransforms. Whoever ports pronunciations.ts needs to make PronunciationTransform callable, or tts_service.ts must be changed to match. | UNRESOLVED: Reviewed with no divergences found: the 15 empty-__init__ index.ts files (services ultravox/whisper/xai/xtts; transports daily/heygen/lemonslice/livekit/local/moq/smallwebrtc/tavus/vonage/websocket/whatsapp), turns/user_mute/index.ts, the base/always/first_speech user mute strategies, empty_user_turn.ts, eager_end_of_turn_mixin.ts, user_idle_controller.ts, user_turn_controller.ts, video_in_sampler.ts, and the rest of tts_service.ts, including WebsocketTTSService and InterruptibleTTSService. Deprecated APIs are correctly left out, broad catches call rethrowIfCancelled, and TimeoutError-only catches rethrow everything else.
- `pipecat.turns.user_turn_processor`: UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/services/tts_service.ts _transformText: upstream PronunciationTransform is a class with an async `__call__`, called directly as `await transform(text, text_type)`. If it is ported as a plain class with a method (not a callable function object), the call fails and the types reject passing `pronunciationTransformIpa()` output in textTransforms. Whoever ports pronunciations.ts needs to make PronunciationTransform callable, or tts_service.ts must be changed to match. | UNRESOLVED: Reviewed with no divergences found: the 15 empty-__init__ index.ts files (services ultravox/whisper/xai/xtts; transports daily/heygen/lemonslice/livekit/local/moq/smallwebrtc/tavus/vonage/websocket/whatsapp), turns/user_mute/index.ts, the base/always/first_speech user mute strategies, empty_user_turn.ts, eager_end_of_turn_mixin.ts, user_idle_controller.ts, user_turn_controller.ts, video_in_sampler.ts, and the rest of tts_service.ts, including WebsocketTTSService and InterruptibleTTSService. - `pipecat.utils.context.llm_context_summarization`: Dropped the deprecated LLMContextSummarizationConfig (and its to_auto_config). Standard messages are read as dicts (snake_case wire keys: tool_calls, tool_call_id). estimateTokens counts code points, as Python len() does. A null content is formatted as 'None', matching upstream str(None). In the PAIR name lookbehind and in KNOWN_TOKENS (`\b`, `github_pat_\w{82,}`), a credential name or token right after a non-ASCII letter can be redacted in JS where Python would leave it. - `pipecat.utils.file_storage`: Adds a TS-only FileNotFoundError (code ENOENT) for Python's builtin. LocalFileStorage takes {folder, maxFiles, fs}: core has no filesystem, so disk I/O goes through an injected LocalFileSystem (a subset of node:fs/promises, which fits as is); without fs, save/load/delete throw. ENOENT from load becomes FileNotFoundError.

- `pipecat.utils.security.ssrf`: Includes a small port of the Python ipaddress features this module needs: IPNetwork (CIDR with strict host bits, contains()), ipAddress() and ipAddressIsGlobal(), using the IANA private and shared registries and judging IPv4-mapped IPv6 by its IPv4 address, as Python 3.13 does. Host resolution is pluggable (setHostResolver). The default resolves IP literals directly and uses node:dns through process.getBuiltinModule when the runtime has it, with no static node: import. A host that cannot be resolved is BLOCKED, as with upstream's OSError. allowedNetworks takes IPNetwork[]. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/utils/security/redaction.ts: JS `\w` and `\b` only match ASCII, while Python's match Unicode. - `pipecat.utils.tracing.service_decorators`: Exports tracedTts, tracedStt, tracedLlm, tracedGeminiLive(op), tracedOpenaiRealtime(op), plus installTracedTts/installTracedStt (the equivalent of upstream's __set_name__, for Node, which strips types and runs no decorator syntax) and the _get*/_add* helpers. DIVERGENCE: upstream checks tracing availability at import time; here the wrappers call isTracingAvailable() each time they run, because registerOpenTelemetry() happens at runtime. The decorators work as TC39 decorators (class-level patches go on each instance via addInitializer), as legacy experimentalDecorators, or as plain calls. start_as_current_span is emulated with startSpan plus api.context.with when the API provides it; exceptions are recorded with recordException/setStatus(ERROR) if the span has them. Span start/end times are passed as HrTime. Temporary pushFrame/startLlmUsageMetrics overrides in tracedLlm are restored by deleting the own property. Gemini Live messages are read with camelCase protocol fields (toolCall.functionCalls, usageMetadata.*TokenCount, serverContent.turnComplete). OpenAI Realtime events keep snake_case wire fields; _sessionProperties uses modelDump() if present, otherwise a shallow copy. JSON.stringify output uses compact separators, unlike Python json.dumps. Depends on unported service methods by convention name: TTS createAudioContext/appendToAudioContext/removeAudioContext/onAudioContextCompleted/resetActiveAudioContext/_audioContextManager.playingContextId; STT vadEnabled/_lastTranscriptTime. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/workers/base_ui_worker.ts does not exist. - `pipecat.utils.tracing.setup`: @opentelemetry/api is an optional peer of @pipedog/core (upstream's [tracing] extra). Upstream's import-time try-import becomes a dynamic import("@opentelemetry/api") started when the module loads (no top-level await; a missing package is caught), so isTracingAvailable() turns true a moment after load; loadOpenTelemetry() awaits it. registerOpenTelemetry({ api, sdk, resources }) registers explicitly and synchronously and wins over the automatic import (@pipedog/tracing's setupTracing() does this). setupTracing({ serviceName, exporter, consoleExport }) needs sdk and resources registered and uses the sdk-trace-base v2 API (BasicTracerProvider with spanProcessors, resourceFromAttributes, trace.setGlobalTracerProvider). The OTel types are structural interfaces (OtelSpan, OtelSpanContext, OtelContext, OtelTracer), so the published types do not need the peer. Upstream's print() of setup errors is logger.error. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/utils/security/redaction.ts: JS `\w` and `\b` only match ASCII, while Python's match Unicode. In the PAIR name lookbehind and in KNOWN_TOKENS (`\b`, `github_pat_\w{82,}`), a credential name or token right after a non-ASCII letter can be redacted in JS where Python would leave it. This only over-redacts and is harmless, so I left it unchanged. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/workers/base_worker.ts checkWorkerNames: the TypeError message names JS types ("number") where Python names its own ("int"). It only affects the error text.

- `pipecat.workers.base_worker`: Not ported (deprecated): the per-argument job spelling (name, payload, timeout, cancel_on_error on requestJob, job, requestJobGroup, jobGroup, createJobGroupAndRequestJob) and resolve_job_params. These methods take { params } only. BaseWorker extends BusSubscriber(BaseObject) (mixin). Methods that take *names plus keyword arguments take rest arguments and an optional trailing options object: addWorkers(a, b, { watch }), requestJobGroup('w1', 'w2', { params }), jobGroup(...). A non-string worker name throws TypeError. _waitWorkersReady returns a function to race with waitFor rather than a Future, because awaiting an async function that returns a Task would wait for the Task. A stable bound handler (_onWatchedWorkerReadyHandler) keeps registry watches idempotent, as Python's bound-method equality does. activeJobs and jobGroups are Maps; JobGroup.responses is a Record. WorkerActivationArgs.fromDict/toDict work from the instance's own fields. Cancelling a job handler cancels its Task, so an await on something other than an asyncio primitive inside a user handler stops only if it is passed the task's signal or wrapped in abortable(). | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/utils/security/redaction.ts: JS `\w` and `\b` only match ASCII, while Python's match Unicode. - `pipecat.workers.llm.backend_llm_worker`: DIVERGENCE: upstream overrides PipelineWorker.capabilities with a plain dict, and TS does not allow an override of a different type, so it is exposed as `backendCapabilities` (the attach update still sends it under the `capabilities` key). The @job handlers attach_frontend/handle_message are attachFrontend/handleMessage, registered in a static block. _BackendSession's async with becomes enter()/exit()/run(fn), and its async for becomes for-await with next(). In _ask, if the job fails before it starts, the response falls back to null (upstream would raise UnboundLocalError there). Calls llm.cancelFunctionCalls({reason}), which now exists in llm_service.ts. Payloads keep their snake_case wire keys. | UNRESOLVED: Minor truthiness edge cases left as they are, judged not worth the churn: UIWorker._screenJob uses `value ? - `pipecat.workers.llm.llm_worker`: Dropped as deprecated: the messages/result_callback params of end() and activate_worker(), and _finish_function_call (used only by those params). DIVERGENCE: core has no context variables, so the _IN_TOOL_CALL ContextVar is a per-task WeakMap keyed by currentTask(). Tasks a tool handler spawns (gather/createTask) do not inherit it, so frames they queue are not deferred. Tools are registered with the public llm.registerFunction plus a bound DirectFunctionWrapper subclass instead of the private _register_direct_function, because a bound or wrapping function loses the source its parameter schema is parsed from. It is still an explicit registration, so advertised tools never auto-register over it. PipelineWorker accepts `active` in TS, so it is passed through options. | UNRESOLVED: Minor truthiness edge cases left as they are, judged not worth the churn: UIWorker._screenJob uses `value ? - `pipecat.workers.proxy`: Re-exports WebSocketProxyClient and WebSocketProxyServer from ./websocket/index.ts. | UNRESOLVED: All transforms (currency, dates, numbers, percentages, phone, units): Python's `\d` in str patterns matches any Unicode decimal digit, and int()/float() accept those digits. The TS ports use ASCII-only `\d` with Number/BigInt. So text like Arabic-Indic '٥٠%' is expanded upstream but left alone in pedog. This is an edge case and fixing it needs digit normalization in 6 files, so I left it alone instead of widening scope. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/workers/proxy/websocket/client.ts: upstream's _receive_loop swallows CancelledError, so the task ends normally. The port rethrows it, which follows the conventions, and says so in the file's porting notes. Every other part of the client matches upstream line by line: events, defaults, forward-message filtering, ConnectionClosed handling and rethrowIfCancelled in the broad catch. server.ts is not ported yet, so the WebSocketProxyServer re-export in websocket/index.ts will fail to resolve until it is.

- `pipecat.workers.proxy.websocket.client`: WebSocketProxyClient(options: {name, url, remoteWorkerName, localWorkerName, forwardMessages?, headers?, serializer?, active? = false}); websockets client replaced by websocketConnect from services/websocket_service.ts. Cancellation handled as upstream (receive loop swallows CancelledError).
- `pipecat.workers.runner`: Ported to /Users/pol/vscode/pipedog/packages/core/src/workers/runner.ts. Dropped deprecated APIs: the `loop` constructor option and the `run(worker)` positional parameter. `run()` now takes `{ autoEnd }`. Divergences: (1) SIGINT/SIGTERM handlers are attached with `globalThis.process.on` only when the runtime has it (Bun, Node, Deno), and do nothing on Workers. (2) `_gcCollect` calls the global `gc()` only if the runtime exposes one (e.g. `--expose-gc`). It cannot log a collected count or `gc.garbage`. (3) The default name uses `crypto.randomUUID()`. The two `except asyncio.CancelledError: pass` blocks catch only CancelledError and rethrow anything else. The runSetupHook name stays `setup_worker_runner`, as upstream; the startup port also accepts the camelCase name. Remaining tsc errors are not in this file: `../pipeline/worker.ts` (PipelineWorker) and `./base_worker.ts` (BaseWorker, WorkerParams) are not written yet, and a TS6305 error says the `@pipedog/asyncio` dist build is stale. The port expects `BaseWorker.attach({ registry, bus, workerRunner })`, `worker.run(new WorkerParams({ taskManager }))`, the `parent`, `active`, `bridged` and `startedAt` getters, and `PipelineWorker.stopWhenDone()`. | UNRESOLVED: None in runner.ts. The rest of the port matches upstream: every method and branch, the on_ready and on_error event registrations, defaults (`||` is kept only where Python's `or` should also skip an empty string), cancellation (CancelledError is swallowed in run() and _runWorker as upstream does, and there are no other broad catches), gather with return_exceptions mapped to gatherSettled, and the deprecated `loop` parameter and `run(worker)` left out. Signals and garbage collection behave differently from Python because JavaScript has no equivalent, and both are marked DIVERGENCE in the code. | UNRESOLVED: Typecheck can't fully pass yet. ../pipeline/worker.ts and ./base_worker.ts are not ported, and the @pipedog/asyncio dist build is stale. This is expected while other modules are ported at the same time. | UNRESOLVED: The relayed user note says Effect could help with stopping work when a user interrupts. That is a decision about the core's architecture and was out of scope for this review. In runner.ts, cancellation already goes through @pipedog/asyncio Tasks (createTask, gatherSettled, Event.wait), so the note asks for no change here.
- `pipecat.workers.ui`: index.ts re-exports the BusUI* messages, uiEvent, UI_STATE_PROMPT_GUIDE, screenTools, UISelection and UIWorker. The deprecated BaseUIWorker and ReplyToolMixin re-exports are dropped. The only tsc error left is the missing ./ui_worker.ts, which another agent is porting. | UNRESOLVED: packages/core/src/workers/base_ui_worker.ts does not exist. This is correct: the whole upstream module is marked @deprecated (since 1.12.0, removed in 2.0.0), so by convention it is not ported. workers/ui/index.ts already leaves out the deprecated BaseUIWorker and ReplyToolMixin re-exports and says so. The manifest entry for this module is still status 'todo' with empty notes; I was told not to edit porting/manifest.json, so someone else needs to record it there as deprecated and not ported. | UNRESOLVED: Checked and left unchanged: pattern_pair_aggregator.ts, phonemes.ts, simple_text_aggregator.ts, skip_tags_aggregator.ts, word_timestamp_utils.ts, service_attributes.ts, workers/llm/index.ts and workers/ui/index.ts match upstream. The only differences are tiny Unicode-class edge cases: Python isspace()/isalnum() against JS \s and \p{L}\p{N}, and Python strip() against JS trim().
